Abstract: A search is presented for massive narrow-width resonances in the mass range of $1\text{-}4.5\,\text{TeV}$ decaying into pairs of Higgs bosons (HH), using proton-proton collision data at a center-of-mass energy of $13\,\text{TeV}$ collected with the CMS detector at the LHC during the $2016\text{-}2018$ data-taking. The data correspond to an integrated luminosity of $138~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$. The analysis targets final states where one Higgs boson decays into a pair of bottom quarks and the other into a pair of tau leptons, $\mathrm{X}\rightarrow\mathrm{HH}\rightarrow \mathrm{b}\bar{\mathrm{b}}\,τ^{+}τ^{-}$. The observed data are found to be consistent with standard model background expectations. Upper limits at $95\%$ confidence level (CL) are set on the production cross section for resonant HH production for masses between $1$ and $4.5\,\text{TeV}$. This analysis sets the most sensitive LHC limits to date on $\mathrm{X}\rightarrow\mathrm{HH}\rightarrow \mathrm{b}\bar{\mathrm{b}}\,τ^{+}τ^{-}$ decays in the mass range of $1.4$ to $4.5\,\text{TeV}$.

</details>

Abstract: Beyond $B$ physics, charm and $τ$ physics constitute a central part of the Belle and Belle II physics programs. Here, we present recent results on charm baryon decays, including first measurements and observations of several previously unmeasured modes, together with new studies in $τ$ physics. Particular emphasis is placed on searches for CP violation in $τ$ decays and lepton-flavor-violating processes. In this context, a search for CP violation in $τ\to πK_{S} ν_τ$ decays is reported for the first time.

Abstract: A search for Higgs boson (H) production in association with two vector bosons (V = W, Z) via vector boson scattering (VBS) is presented using proton-proton collision data collected at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV by the CMS experiment, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. Events containing two forward jets consistent with VBS, a large-radius jet from the decay of a boosted H to a pair of b quarks, and 0, 1, or 2 charged leptons coming from V decays are selected. The process is excluded at 95% CL for observed (expected) values of the VVHH coupling modifier $κ_\mathrm{VV}$ outside the interval 0.40 $\lt$ $κ_\mathrm{VV}$ $\lt$ 1.60 (0.34 $\lt$ $κ_\mathrm{VV}$ $\lt$ 1.66), assuming standard model values for all other couplings, thus establishing a novel probe of the VVHH interaction. Constraints are also set on the individual $κ_\mathrm{2W}$ and $κ_\mathrm{2Z}$ coupling modifiers, and on the allowed region in the $κ_\mathrm{2W}$-$κ_\mathrm{2Z}$ plane.

</details>

### [85] [Stochastic reversal of deterministic selection in epidemic strain competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22876)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典确定性流行病学理论认为基本再生数（R0）完全决定菌株间的竞争选择，但忽略随机效应。实际中不同菌株竞争易感人群是重要问题，需要理解随机性在其中扮演的角色。

Method: 建立两菌株SIR模型，结合确定性分析和随机动力学分析（噪声驱动），将竞争解释为围绕有效势的动态演化，势垒对应共存态的不稳定流形。

Result: 1) 随机波动可逆转确定性优势，即使在远离准中性区域也能发生；2) 随机性将固定时间从年尺度（确定性情形）大幅缩短至天尺度；3) 固定时间与噪声强度及距准中性区域距离呈非线性关系，遵循普适标度律。

Conclusion: 随机效应在菌株竞争中不可忽略，噪声诱导的势垒穿越可以导致优势逆转，即使在稳定主导状态下也有显著概率发生，需要通过随机框架而非纯确定性理论来理解菌株竞争动态。

Abstract: Different strains competing for a common pool of susceptible individuals is a key problem in mathematical epidemiology. To address this problem, we investigate a two-strain model within a Susceptible-Infected-Recovered (SIR) framework. While classical deterministic theory predicts that the basic reproduction number fully determines selection, we show that stochastic effects play a key role in the dynamics. We discover that stochastic fluctuations can reverse the deterministic advantage even far from the quasi-neutral regime. Further, we find that stochasticity drastically reduces fixation times from years, in the deterministic case, to days. The fixation time is non-linearly proportional to the noise intensity and the distance from the quasi-neutral regime, following a universal rule obtained from a scaling law. The nature of the problem and the equations allow us to interpret the competition as a dynamical evolution around an effective potential, with the potential barrier corresponding to the unstable manifold associated with the coexistence. Even in a stable situation of dominance of one strain, the noise can induce crossings through the potential. We find that the reversal can occur even far from the quasi-neutral regime with significant probability.

</details>


### [86] [Polynomial-time completion of phylogenetic tree sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23984)
*Aleksandr Koshkarov,Nadia Tahiri*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际系统发育研究中，不同树通常包含部分重叠但不完全相同的分类单元集合。传统方法剪除非共享叶节点会丢失系统发育信号，而现有补全方法难以同时保持拓扑和分支长度信息。需要一种高效算法来处理重叠分类单元树的集合级补全。

Method: 算法包括：(1) 识别并提取源树间频繁出现的最大补全子树；(2) 构建加权多数规则共识树；(3) 利用共有叶节点推导速率来缩放分支长度；(4) 将每个共识子树插入到目标树原始分支上使二次距离误差最小化的位置。算法运行时间为多项式时间，且处理顺序无关。

Result: 在两栖动物、哺乳动物、鲨鱼和鳞龙类数据集上的实验表明，该方法在所有子集上均实现了与参考树的最低距离，在拓扑和分支长度两方面均优于对比方法。算法保持原始分类单元间的距离，输出唯一的补全结果。

Conclusion: 提出了一种多项式时间、顺序无关、能保持距离信息的系统发育树集合补全算法，在真实生物数据集上表现优异，并提供了开源实现。

Abstract: Comparative analyses of phylogenetic trees typically require identical taxon sets, however, in practice, trees often include distinct but overlapping taxa. Pruning non-shared leaves discards phylogenetic signal, whereas tree completion can preserve both taxa and branch-length information. This work introduces a polynomial-time algorithm for set-wide completion of phylogenetic trees with partial taxon overlap. The proposed method identifies and extracts maximal completion subtrees that frequently appear across the source trees and constructs a weighted majority-rule consensus. Branch lengths are scaled using rates derived from common leaves. Each consensus subtree is inserted at the position that minimizes the quadratic distance error measured against information from the source trees, with candidate positions restricted to the original branches of the target tree. We demonstrate that the algorithm runs in polynomial time and preserves distances among the original taxa, yielding a unique completion that is order-independent with respect to the processing order of target trees. An experimental evaluation on amphibians, mammals, sharks, and squamates shows that the proposed method consistently achieves the lowest distance to the subset reference trees across subsets among all methods, in both topology and branch lengths.
  An open-source Python implementation of the proposed algorithm and the biological datasets utilized in this study are publicly available at: https://github.com/tahiri-lab/overlap-treeset-completion/.

</details>

### [87] [The U(1)-topological elliptic genus is surjective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22949)
*Tilman Bauer,Mayuko Yamashita*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了SU流形的配边环到拓扑Jacobi形式的拓扑椭圆亏格可以提升到连通拓扑Jacobi形式，并且该提升在同伦意义上是满射的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将拓扑椭圆亏格从配边环提升到连通拓扑Jacobi形式，并研究其同伦满射性。

Method: 通过构造从SU流形配边环到连通拓扑Jacobi形式的提升映射，并证明该映射在同伦群上是满射。

Result: 该提升映射存在且在同伦意义下是满射。

Conclusion: 拓扑椭圆亏格可以自然地提升到连通拓扑Jacobi形式，且该提升具有同伦满射性，这进一步丰富了拓扑Jacobi形式与SU流形配边理论之间的联系。

Abstract: We show that the topological elliptic genus from the cobordism ring of SU-manifolds to topological Jacobi forms lifts to connective topological Jacobi forms, and that this lift is surjective in homotopy.

</details>


### [88] [Rational relative sectional category](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23395)
*Lekha Das,Bittu Singh*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文在有理同伦论中利用交换微分分次代数（CDGAs）为连续映射的相对截面范畴建立了代数模型。主要结果表明，对于形式映射，有理相对截面范畴可以纯粹由上同调通过理想幂零性计算，并证明该等式在一般拓扑情境下可能不成立。应用该框架，获得了映射的有理Lusternik-Schnirelmann范畴和有理高阶拓扑复杂性的纯代数刻画，最后给出了形式映射间有理同伦距离的代数描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将相对截面范畴这一拓扑不变量在有理同伦论框架下代数化，以利用CDGA工具进行更简便的计算，并探究形式映射条件下该不变量与上同调代数结构的关系。

Method: 采用交换微分分次代数（CDGAs）建立代数模型，通过理想幂零性刻画有理相对截面范畴，并针对形式映射情形给出上同调层面的计算方法。

Result: 建立了形式映射的有理相对截面范畴等于上同调理想幂零性；证明了该等式在一般拓扑中不成立；给出了有理L-S范畴、有理高阶拓扑复杂性和有理同伦距离的纯代数刻画。

Conclusion: 为有理同伦论中的截面类不变量提供了统一的代数化框架，揭示了形式映射下拓扑不变量与代数结构之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We develop an algebraic model for the relative sectional category of a continuous map in rational homotopy theory using commutative differential graded algebras (CDGAs). Our main result establishes that for formal maps, the rational relative sectional category can be computed purely from cohomology, using ideal nilpotency. We also show that this equality may fail in general topological settings. Applying this framework, we obtain purely algebraic characterizations for the rational Lusternik-Schnirelmann category and the rational higher topological complexity of a map. Finally, we provide an algebraic description of the rational homotopic distance between formal maps.

</details>


### [89] [Gyration Stability for Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24301)
*Sebastian Chenery*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Poincaré对偶复形上的回转操作（gyration），该操作源于给定复形N与球面的乘积上的某种手术，并依赖选择的扭转参数。特别关注回转稳定性（gyration stability）概念，即当N的所有回转（无论使用何种扭转）都具有相同的同伦类型时，称N是回转稳定的。本文证明，当两个Poincaré对偶复形的乘积N×M中有一个因子是回转稳定的，则该乘积也是回转稳定的，并给出了一些感兴趣的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Poincaré对偶复形在回转操作下的稳定性条件，特别是乘积结构如何继承稳定性性质。

Method: 通过代数拓扑和同伦论方法，分析Poincaré对偶复形乘积的回转操作，证明稳定性在乘积下的继承性。

Result: 证明了若N×M中有一个因子是回转稳定的，则整个乘积也是回转稳定的。

Conclusion: 乘积结构不破坏回转稳定性，且提供了具体实例支撑该结论。

Abstract: A gyration is an operation on Poincaré Duality complexes that arises from a certain surgery on the product of a given complex $N$ and a sphere, parametrised by a chosen twisting. Of particular recent interest is the notion of gyration stability; that is, $N$ is gyration stable when all of its gyrations have the same homotopy type, regardless of the twisting used. We prove that a product $N\times M$ of two Poincaré Duality complexes is gyration stable when one of the product terms is itself gyration stable, and provide some examples of interest.

</details>


### [90] [A rational model for the fiberwise THH transfer II: $A_\infty$-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24709)
*Florian Naef,Robin Stoll*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文为两部分系列论文的第二部分，第一部分证明了纤维wise THH转移的有理模型由cdga模型的Hochschild同调转移给出。本文则利用A∞-代数给出该Hochschild同调转移的显式描述，推广了Bouc的工作。借助Lind-Malkiewich的结果推导出Becker-Gottlieb转移的有理模型，并将其应用于流形拓扑：一方面证明Berglund构造的有理特征类中对应非三价单环图的部分在纤维为紧单连通拓扑流形的纤维丛上消失；另一方面给出纤维wise THH-单结构空间的有理模型，为在有理协边稳定范围内获得紧单连通流形的微分同胚群与同胚群分类空间的有理模型奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为纤维wise THH转移提供显式的A∞-代数描述，推广Bouc的工作，并应用所得结果研究流形拓扑中的有理特征类与分类空间模型。

Method: 利用A∞-代数理论给出Hochschild同调转移的显式描述；结合Lind-Malkiewich的结果推导Becker-Gottlieb转移的有理模型；通过Lie图复形分析Berglund的有理特征类在纤维丛上的消没问题。

Result: 证明了对应非三价单环图的Berglund有理特征类在纤维为紧单连通拓扑流形的纤维丛上消没；给出了纤维wise THH-单结构空间的有理模型。

Conclusion: 建立了THH转移与Becker-Gottlieb转移之间的有理模型联系，并为后续获得紧单连通流形的微分同胚群与同胚群分类空间的有理模型提供了关键步骤。

Abstract: In Part I, we proved that a rational model for the fiberwise THH transfer of a map $f$ of fibrations over a base space is given by the Hochschild homology transfer of a cdga model of $f$. In this paper, we provide an explicit description of this Hochschild homology transfer in terms of $A_\infty$-algebras, generalizing work of Bouc. Using a result of Lind-Malkiewich, we deduce a rational model for the Becker-Gottlieb transfer. We furthermore use our results for the following applications to manifold topology.
  Firstly, we consider the rational characteristic classes constructed by Berglund for fibrations with fiber a Poincaré complex (which generalize classes found by Berglund-Madsen); they are defined via the Lie graph complex, and we prove that the classes corresponding to non-trivalent graphs with exactly one loop vanish when evaluated on fiber bundles with fiber a compact simply connected topological manifold.
  Secondly, we provide a rational model for the space of fiberwise THH-simple structures, which is a step towards obtaining rational models for the classifying spaces of diffeomorphisms and homeomorphisms of a compact simply connected manifold in the rational concordance stable range.

</details>


### [91] [Homology-based Morphometry of Brain Atrophy: Methods and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24714)
*Donato Quiccione,Mariam Pirashvili,Nathan Broomhead,Sean J. Fallon*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于持续同调(PH)的拓扑数据分析流程，用于量化结构T1加权MRI的多尺度几何特征。流程1通过切片级欧几里得距离变换量化区域性皮质变薄，适合跨群体分析；流程2使用α-滤过测量扫描间的结构相似性，捕捉脑沟增宽和脑室扩大，适合纵向追踪。在ADNI数据上，流程1实现AD与CN分类的ROC-AUC=0.895（无需非线性配准），流程2有效捕获疾病相关的纵向变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于体素的形态学分析（如VBM）需要图像标准化到标准模板，这可能会模糊受试者特异性的几何特征，且在比较存在严重脑病理、病灶或其他解剖异常的群体时存在问题。因此，需要一种不依赖非线性配准、能保留个体几何特征的结构脑影像分析方法。

Method: 引入两个基于持续同调的互补流程：流程1采用切片级欧几里得距离变换量化组织掩膜的局部厚度变化；流程2使用α-滤过（alpha filtrations）计算扫描对之间的结构相似性，以捕捉皮质表面形态变化。

Result: 合成实验表明流程1适合跨受试者分析，流程2适合受试者内设计。在ADNI真实数据中，流程1以单模态T1加权MRI（无需非线性配准）实现AD与CN分类，ROC-AUC=0.895，峰值效应位于内侧颞叶区域；流程2捕获了疾病相关的纵向变化，AD受试者表现出比CN更大的短期变化。

Conclusion: 这两个流程为横断面群体比较和纵向追踪提供了可解释的拓扑生物标志物，弥补了传统VBM方法在个体几何特征保留方面的不足。

Abstract: Understanding the structure of the brain, and how it changes with time and disease, is a core goal of structural neuroimaging. Contemporary approaches to structural brain analysis are dominated by voxel-wise, mass-univariate methods such as voxel-based morphometry (VBM). However, these techniques require images to be normalized to a standard template, which can obscure subject-specific geometric features. Normalization to a common stereotactic space can also be problematic when comparing groups with substantial brain pathology, lesions, or other anatomical abnormalities.
  Here, we introduce two complementary pipelines based on persistent homology (PH), a tool from topological data analysis, to quantify multiscale geometric features of structural T1-weighted MRI scans. Pipeline 1 quantifies regional thinning by applying the Euclidean distance transform to tissue masks in a slice-wise manner. Pipeline 2 uses \(α\)-filtrations to measure structural similarity between pairs of scans, capturing sulcal widening and ventricular enlargement.
  Synthetic experiments with controlled induced lesions showed that Pipeline 1 is best suited to between-subject analyses, whereas Pipeline 2 is better suited to within-subject designs. Applied to real-world data from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI), Pipeline 1 separated Alzheimer's disease (AD) from cognitively normal (CN) participants using single-modality T1-weighted MRI without nonlinear registration (ROC-AUC = 0.895), with peak effects localized to medial temporal regions. Pipeline 2 captured disease-related longitudinal change, with follow-up scans remaining closest to their own baselines and AD subjects showing greater short-interval change than CN subjects. Together, these pipelines provide interpretable topological biomarkers for cross-sectional group comparisons and longitudinal tracking.

</details>

### [92] [Cyclic fractional Gaussian noise: time and frequency domain properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22813)
*Hubert Woszczek,Agnieszka Wylomanska*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出循环分数高斯噪声(cfGn)模型，通过将二维分数布朗运动的增量与周期确定性函数调制相结合，同时实现了二阶循环平稳性与长程依赖特性，并严格推导了其自协方差函数和循环谱等性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典循环平稳过程无法刻画复杂经验数据中存在的持久、慢衰减相关性（长程依赖），因此需要一种能够同时建模循环平稳性和长记忆特性的随机模型。

Method: 扩展振幅调制平稳框架，利用二维分数布朗运动(2d fBm)的增量作为驱动过程，通过周期确定性函数调制2d fGn的单变量坐标并求和两个分量来构建cfGn模型。

Result: 理论推导和蒙特卡洛模拟表明，cfGn在保持ACVF周期行为的同时继承了长记忆特性，这在时域和频域均得到体现。

Conclusion: 该框架为信号基的状态监测提供了稳健基础，尤其适用于周期性故障分量与长程依赖背景噪声共存的系统。

Abstract: This article introduces cyclic fractional Gaussian noise (cfGn), a stochastic model that integrates second-order cyclostationarity with long-range dependence property. While classical cyclostationary processes are widely discussed in the literature, they often lack the capacity to account for the persistent, slow-decaying correlations found in complex empirical data. To bridge this gap, we extend the amplitude-modulated stationary framework by utilizing increments of two-dimensional fractional Brownian motion (2d fBm) as the underlying driving process. The proposed cfGn model is constructed by summing two components, which include periodic deterministic functions modulating the univariate coordinates of 2d fGn. We provide a rigorous derivation of the considered model's properties, specifically the autocovariance function (ACVF) and frequency-domain characteristics, including the cyclic spectrum. Through theoretical considerations of asymptotic properties and Monte Carlo simulations, we demonstrate that cfGn preserves periodic behavior of ACVF while inheriting long-memory traits which is manifested in time and frequency domains. This framework offers a robust foundation, for instance, in signal-based condition monitoring in systems where periodic fault components coexist with long-range dependent background noise.

</details>


### [93] [Come Together: Analyzing Popular Songs Through Statistical Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22925)
*Matthew Esmaili Mallory,Mark Glickman,Jason Brown*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出基于逻辑主成分分析（LPCA）的方法，将流行歌曲的复杂结构（和弦、旋律、音高转换等）嵌入为实值向量，实现对Lennon和McCartney早期（1962-1966）作品的多变量统计分析，探究歌曲聚类、风格演变及两位作曲家的创作趋同/分化趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流行音乐歌曲结构复杂，传统统计工具难以直接分析。为克服这一障碍，需要将非标准化的音乐数据对象转换为可进行标准多变量分析的数值向量表示。

Method: 采用逻辑主成分分析（Logistic Principal Component Analysis）从全局歌曲特征（和弦、旋律音符、和弦与音高转换、旋律轮廓）构建嵌入向量，随后对嵌入进行标准多变量统计分析。

Result: 成功将Lennon-McCartney歌曲嵌入低维空间，揭示了歌曲按披头士专辑的聚类模式、创作风格随时间的演化，以及两位作曲家作品风格的趋同或分化趋势。

Conclusion: 基于LPCA的嵌入方法为统计检验流行音乐的结构与风格发展提供了一个强有力的分析框架。

Abstract: Statistical modeling of popular music presents a unique challenge due to the complexity of song structures, which cannot be easily analyzed using conventional statistical tools. However, recent advances in data science have shown that converting non-standard data objects into real vector-valued embeddings enables meaningful statistical analysis. In this work, we demonstrate an approach based on logistic principal component analysis to construct embeddings from global song features, allowing for standard multivariate analysis. We apply this method to a corpus of Lennon and McCartney songs from 1962-1966, using embeddings derived from chords, melodic notes, chord and pitch transitions, and melodic contours. Our analysis explores how these song embeddings cluster by Beatles album, how songwriting styles evolved over time, and whether Lennon and McCartney's compositions exhibited convergence or divergence. This embedding-based approach offers a powerful framework for statistically examining musical structure and stylistic development in popular music.

</details>


### [94] [Estimating Causal Attribution of Anthropogenic Forcing on High-Temperature Extremes Using a Latent Gaussian Spatial Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23438)
*Ritik Roshan Giri,Arnab Hazra*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的因果推断框架，通过双变量广义极值分布和潜在高斯模型，量化人为强迫对美国大陆高温极端事件发生概率的贡献，并利用Max-and-smooth近似贝叶斯推断技术进行高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化导致极端天气事件频发，尤其是高温极端事件，而人为强迫在其中扮演了重要角色。现有方法难以有效量化人为强迫对极端事件的具体贡献，因此需要一种新的因果推断框架来分离和评估这种影响。

Method: 基于IPSL-CM6A模型的CMIP6实验数据，构建事实世界与反事实世界的年度温度最大值。采用双变量广义极值分布联合建模两个世界的年度最大值，利用多元内在条件自回归模型对空间变化系数进行建模，并通过Max-and-smooth技术（拉普拉斯近似+吉布斯采样）进行高效近似贝叶斯推断。

Result: 给出了人为强迫对高温极端事件的因果效应的后验估计，以及该效应在事实世界中的趋势。同时估计了显著因果效应的可信区域，用于在美国大陆范围内检测热点区域。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效量化人为强迫对高温极端事件的影响，并为极端事件归因分析提供了新的方法论支持，有助于识别受人为气候变化影响最显著的区域。

Abstract: Climate change has become a significant global concern due to its capacity to cause substantial disruption to daily life by increasing the frequency and intensity of extreme weather events. Given the rising trend of human interventions in the climate system over recent decades, this study aims to quantify the relative contribution of anthropogenic forcing to the increasing likelihood of climate extremes, with a particular emphasis on high-temperature extremes. Our analysis focuses on annual temperature maxima from the IPSL-CM6A model in the CMIP6 experiment. We propose a novel causal inference framework that focuses on differences in return levels derived from annual temperature maxima between the factual and counterfactual worlds. While jointly modeling the annual maxima from the two worlds using a bivariate generalized extreme value distribution, we model the spatially-varying coefficients using a latent Gaussian framework. Specifically, given that the data are available over a $1^\circ \times 1^\circ$ grid, we employ the multivariate intrinsic conditional autoregressive model for the latent layer in the proposed hierarchical model, ensuring proper posterior distributions. We implement a recently developed highly-efficient approximate Bayesian inference technique, `Max-and-smooth', that uses a Laplace approximation of the likelihood and then performs Gibbs sampling based on the approximate posterior. The results include posterior estimates of the causal effect of anthropogenic forcing on high-temperature extremes, along with the trends in this effect, over the factual world. Furthermore, we estimate credible regions for a significant causal effect to facilitate hotspot detection across the mainland United States.

</details>


### [95] [How temperature regimes near the equinox synchronize spring biological events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23744)
*Jonathan Auerbach,Andrew Gelman,E. M. Wolkovich*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用停止随机游走理论证明，基础热和模型（无需引入额外复杂因子）即可解释气候变暖下春季物候事件敏感性下降和同步性减弱的现象，并通过丁香花等数据验证了预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着气候变暖，春季物候事件的时间提前幅度减缓（敏感性下降），事件发生时间的变异性增加（同步性减弱），这引发了对基于温度的物候协调机制在人为气候变化下韧性的质疑。本文旨在探究是否需要通过引入额外因素和机制来复杂化热和模型以解释这些现象。

Method: 利用停止随机游走理论分析基本热和模型，推导出温度与生物事件时间及同步性之间的非线性关系；通过实验和真实世界数据（包括美国1956-2025年10,000次丁香花观测）验证理论预测。

Result: 基础热和模型精确预测了敏感性下降和同步性减弱的现象。理论表明，随着温度升高，春季事件从春分点向夏至点推移，事件本身变得协调性更差、变异性更大，这些预测得到了实验和真实数据的支持。

Conclusion: 停止随机游走理论为理解热和模型提供了有力工具，在考虑额外复杂性时尤其如此；说明无需复杂化模型即可解释观察到的物候变化模式。

Abstract: Many biological processes, including plant leafout and flowering, occur once cumulative temperatures reach a threshold (the thermal-sum model). In this way, temperatures are thought to coordinate the timing of biological events. But growing evidence suggests that as climates warm, both the advancement of spring has slowed (declining sensitivity) and the variance in the timing of spring events has increased (declining synchrony), raising questions about the resilience of temperature-based coordination to anthropogenic climate change. To answer these questions, researchers have complicated the thermal-sum model, introducing additional factors and mechanisms. We consider whether such complexity is necessary. Using results from the theory of stopped random walks, we show that sensitivity and synchrony are exactly as predicted by the basic thermal-sum model. The theory suggests a nonlinear relationship between temperatures and both the timing and synchrony of biological events. In particular, it predicts that as temperatures increase and springtime events shift from the equinox toward the solstice, the events themselves become less coordinated and more variable. We verify these predictions using experimental and real-world data, including 10,000 observations of common lilacs (United States, 1956-2025). We conclude that the theory provides a powerful tool for understanding the thermal-sum model, particularly when considering additional complexity.

</details>


### [96] [Sparse Reduced-rank Regression Methods for Spatially Misaligned Data with Application to Spatial Transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23755)
*Zitian Wu,Susmita Datta,Arkaprava Roy*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种核加权回归框架，结合稀疏低秩分解，用于分析STARmap PLUS技术生成的高分辨率空间转录组学数据，以揭示阿尔茨海默病中斑块大小与邻近细胞空间转录组之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解疾病进展（如阿尔茨海默病）中时空动态与转录组图谱的关系至关重要，但现有方法缺乏高效整合高分辨率空间转录组学和蛋白检测数据的能力。

Method: 开发核加权回归框架，将斑块大小建模为邻近细胞空间转录组的集体效应，并自动整合跨细胞类型和不同疾病状态的组织样本；引入稀疏低秩分解实现基因选择并跨基因、细胞类型和时间点借力。

Result: 模拟研究证明了该方法在多种设定下的鲁棒性和优越性；应用于阿尔茨海默病数据后，发现了具有生物学意义的关联。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效挖掘疾病机制相关的生物学关联，具有推进疾病机制理解的潜力。

Abstract: Understanding the spatiotemporal dynamics of disease progression in relation to transcriptomic profiles provides key insights into complex conditions such as Alzheimer disease. To enable such investigations, STARmap PLUS technology offers joint profiling of high-resolution spatial transcriptomics and protein detection within the same tissue section. Motivated by data from Zeng et al. (2023), we develop a novel kernel-weighted regression framework that models plaque size as a collective effect of the spatial transcriptomics of neighboring cells, automatically integrating across cell types and tissue samples from different disease states. To further strengthen interpretability and efficiency, we incorporate a sparse low-rank factorization that enables gene selection while borrowing strength across genes, cell types, and time points. The proposed approach is implemented in a fully automated manner with data-driven specification of key model components. Through simulation studies, we demonstrate the robustness of the proposed method and its superiority across a range of specification scenarios. Applied to Alzheimer disease data, the proposed framework uncovers biologically meaningful associations, highlighting its potential for advancing the understanding of disease mechanisms.

</details>


### [97] [Extended State-dependent Hawkes Process for Limit Order Books: Mathematical Foundation and the Reproduction of Volatility Signature Plots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23961)
*Akitoshi Kimura*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出ExsdHawkes模型，通过允许状态消失（高頻交易中常見現象）來建模限價訂單簿的複雜動力學；理論上證明MLE可分離，實證發現該模型能再現波動率特徵圖的上行斜率，並識別可市價化限價訂單（MLO）為觸發不穩定狀態的關鍵因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 傳統Hawkes過程在建模限價訂單簿時，因約束過強而無法捕捉高頻交易中常見的「狀態消失」現象，導致模型在模擬穩定性與宏觀波動率刻畫上存在結構性偏差。

Method: 提出Extended State-Dependent Hawkes Process (ExsdHawkes)，放寬傳統約束允許狀態消失；利用KKT條件證明最大似然估計可分離，從而實現高效的兩步估計流程。

Result: 在三菱UFJ金融集團三個月高頻tick數據上，ExsdHawkes能再現波動率特徵圖的上行斜率，捕捉非均衡狀態下的「局部超臨界性」；識別MLO為LOB進入不穩定狀態的主要催化劑；無物理約束的模型會出現爆炸性分支比並失去模擬穩定性。

Conclusion: 物理一致性並非數學上的裝飾，而是準確建模宏觀波動率的必要條件；通過在不可行期間「暫停」殘差累積來施加物理幾何約束，ExsdHawkes能維持統計完整性，而無約束模型則陷入結構性偏差。

Abstract: This paper proposes an Extended State-Dependent Hawkes Process (ExsdHawkes) to model the intricate dynamics of Limit Order Books (LOBs). Our theoretical contribution lies in relaxing traditional constraints by allowing for state disappearances -- a phenomenon frequently observed in high-frequency trading. We mathematically prove, using Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) conditions, that the maximum likelihood estimation remains separable, justifying an efficient two-step procedure.
  In the empirical section, we apply our model to three months of high-frequency tick data of Mitsubishi UFJ Financial Group (8306). We demonstrate that ExsdHawkes uniquely reproduces the volatility signature plot's characteristic upward slope by capturing the "local super-criticality" triggered during disequilibrium states. Crucially, we identify Marketable Limit Orders (MLO) as the primary catalyst that forces the LOB into these unstable states. Comparative analysis reveals that models lacking physical constraints (e.g., standard SD-Hawkes) suffer from explosive branching ratios and fail to maintain simulation stability. Our findings suggest that physical consistency is not merely a mathematical nicety, but a prerequisite for accurately modeling macro-level volatility. By enforcing the physical geometry to `pause' the residual accumulation during inadmissible periods, ExsdHawkes uniquely maintains statistical integrity where unconstrained models succumb to structural bias.

</details>


### [98] [Bayesian inference for hidden Markov models under genuine multimodality with application to ecological time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24587)
*Marco A. Gallegos-Herrada,Vianey Leos-Barajas,Jeffrey S. Rosenthal*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文讨论了隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）中贝叶斯推理面临的多模态后验分布挑战，指出标准并行回火（PT）算法在HMM中可能效果不佳，提出了改进的PT算法实现方案和新的无信息先验分布，并通过蓝鲸潜水数据分析（含3状态HMM及协变量影响）验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HMM中的似然函数和联合后验分布存在多模态性（即使校正了标签切换问题），标准并行回火算法在高维复杂多模态后验分布中探索效率不足，需要改进算法实现和先验分布设计以有效处理此类问题。

Method: 分析标准PT算法在HMM中的常见陷阱并提出改进方案；引入新的无信息先验分布以促进后验分布的有效探索；采用两个3状态HMM对蓝鲸潜水时间序列进行贝叶斯分析，其中一个HMM在转移概率矩阵中包含分类协变量以评估声音刺激对蓝鲸行为的影响。

Result: 改进后的PT算法能够有效探索HMM中存在的多模态后验分布，并且通过蓝鲸潜水数据的实证分析，展示了改进方法对推断蓝鲸运动模式的实际影响。

Conclusion: 对PT算法的恰当修改和先验分布的合理选择是解决HMM中多模态后验分布探索问题的关键，改进方法能够有效促进后验分布探索并影响对蓝鲸行为模式的推断。

Abstract: Bayesian inference in hidden Markov models (HMMs) can be challenging due to the presence of multimodality in the likelihood function, and consequently in the joint posterior distribution, even after correcting for label switching. The parallel tempering (PT) algorithm, a state-space augmentation method, is a widely used approach for dealing with multimodal distributions. Nevertheless, standard implementation of the PT algorithm may not always be sufficient to effectively explore the high-dimensional, complex multimodal posterior distributions that arise in HMMs. In this work, we demonstrate common pitfalls when implementing the PT algorithm for HMMs, approaches to remedy them, and introduce new non-informative prior distributions that facilitate effective posterior distribution exploration. We analyse time series of blue whale dive data with two 3-state HMMs in a Bayesian framework, one of which includes a categorical covariate in the transition probability matrix to account for the effect of sound stimuli on the whale's behavior. We demonstrate how effective implementation of the modified PT algorithm for Bayesian inference leads to effective exploration of the resultant multimodal posterior distribution and how that affects inference for the underlying movement patterns of the blue whales.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [SwarmDrive: Semantic V2V Coordination for Latency-Constrained Cooperative Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22852)
*Anjie Qiu,Donglin Wang,Zexin Fang,Sanket Partani,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SwarmDrive 利用车车间语义协作，通过小语言模型在不确定性高时共享意图分布，并基于事件触发共识机制融合信息。在遮挡交叉口场景下，相比单车辆SLM（68.9%成功率），6G设置下将成功率提升至94.1%，延迟从云端的510ms降至151.4ms。最优配置为4辆车、熵阈值0.65，但结果仅为原型验证，非6G部署级评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云托管大模型推理存在往返延迟和连接依赖问题，而纯本地边缘模型在遮挡场景下性能不足。现有V2V方法要么通信开销大，要么缺乏语义级别的选择性协作。

Method: 提出SwarmDrive框架：多车辆运行本地小型语言模型（SLM），仅在不确定性高时通过事件触发共识分享紧凑的意图分布，融合多车语义信息实现协同决策。

Result: 在遮挡交叉口5种子实验下，SwarmDrive 6G设置成功率从单车的68.9%提升至94.1%，延迟从云端510ms降至151.4ms。最优群规模为4辆车，熵触发阈值0.65，但更多车辆导致通信开销和丢包增加。

Conclusion: 语义边缘协作在紧迫延迟约束下可有效提升遮挡场景的自动驾驶性能，但当前结果仅为原型验证，不能作为真实6G部署的评估。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted LLM inference for autonomous driving adds round-trip delay and depends on stable connectivity, while purely local edge models struggle under occlusion. We present SwarmDrive, a semantic Vehicle-to-Vehicle (V2V) coordination framework in which nearby vehicles run local Small Language Models (SLMs), share compact intent distributions only when uncertainty is high, and fuse them through event-triggered consensus. We evaluate SwarmDrive in a 5-seed executable study built around one occluded intersection case, combining matched operating-point comparisons with robustness sweeps. In that setting, SwarmDrive under its 6G communication setting ("Swarm 6G") raises success from 68.9% to 94.1% over a single local SLM while reducing latency from a 510 ms cloud reference to 151.4 ms. However, an increased number of participating vehicles leads to higher communication overhead and packet loss. SwarmDrive also evaluates the impact of swarm-size, packet-loss, and entropy-threshold sweeps and shows that the cooperative gain holds across ablations and is best balanced near an active swarm size of 4 vehicles and an entropy trigger threshold of 0.65 in the current prototype. These results show that semantic edge cooperation can work under tight latency constraints in the targeted intersection case, but they are not a deployment-grade validation of a real 6G stack.

</details>


### [100] [Airspeed Forward-Invariance for Unpowered Fixed-Wing Aircraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22860)
*Huseyin Emre Tekaslan,Ella M. Atkins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对无动力固定翼飞行器在稳态风场中通过势能-动能交换调节空速的问题，提出了一种基于生存性理论（Nagumo切向条件）的空速保护方法。该方法推导出风相关的闭合形式许可制导指令，保证空速安全包络的前向不变性，并通过离线二次规划框架在制导层面认证空速安全的机动基元。在高保真无动力固定翼模型上的滑翔轨迹验证表明空速严格有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无动力固定翼飞行器的空速仅通过势能与动能的交换进行调节，因此空速动态对制导指令（特别是飞行路径角指令）高度敏感，且在风场中风险加剧。现有方法缺乏在制导层面显式保证空速安全的闭合形式条件，亟需一种可认证的空速保护方法。

Method: 利用Nagumo切向条件推导稳态风场中空速安全包络前向不变性的闭合形式条件；将该条件嵌入离线二次规划（QP）框架，用于认证非爬升飞行模式下空速安全的机动基元。

Result: 在高保真无动力固定翼模型上，通过串联已认证的安全机动基元构成滑翔轨迹，验证了空速严格有界。

Conclusion: 提出的生存性空速保护方法可在制导层面有效保证无动力固定翼飞行器空速安全，未来工作将拓展至非稳态风场及飞行实验验证。

Abstract: Autonomous fixed-wing flight is becoming a key capability in aerial robotics, enabling sensing, mobility, and contingency operations across both small-scale Uncrewed Aircraft Systems and large-scale Advanced Air Mobility. During unpowered operation in fixed-wing platforms, airspeed is regulated solely through potential-kinetic energy exchange, making airspeed dynamics highly sensitive to guidance commands, particularly under wind. This paper presents a viability-based airspeed protection for ground-referenced guidance in steady wind, where airspeed evolution depends explicitly on the commanded flight path angle. Leveraging Nagumo's tangency condition, we derive a closed-form, wind-dependent characterization of admissible guidance commands that guarantees forward invariance of a safe airspeed envelope. These conditions are embedded within an offline quadratic programming framework to certify airspeed-safe maneuver primitives for non-ascending flight at the guidance level. The approach is validated using a high-fidelity unpowered fixed-wing aircraft model on gliding trajectories formed by concatenating certified maneuver primitives, demonstrating strict airspeed boundedness. Future work will address unsteady wind fields and flight experiments.

</details>


### [101] [RecoverFormer: End-to-End Contact-Aware Recovery for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22911)
*Zihui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RECOVERFORMER，一种全端到端的人形机器人恢复策略，通过因果Transformer和两个新模块（潜在恢复模式和接触可供性头）在扰动下实现多模态、接触感知的稳定恢复，并在零样本迁移和动力学失配下表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在非结构化环境中必须从意外扰动中恢复，但端到端控制策略在这方面仍面临挑战。

Method: 采用因果Transformer处理50步历史观测，结合两个新设计：1) 潜在恢复模式头，实现不同恢复策略间的平滑切换；2) 接触可供性头，预测哪些环境表面有利于稳定。在Unitree G1人形机器人上进行MuJoCo仿真训练。

Result: 在开放地面训练后零样本迁移到有墙环境，100-300N推力下恢复成功率达100%；在零样本动力学失配下（+25%质量、30ms延迟、低摩擦等）达75.5%-99%成功率。

Conclusion: 单一端到端策略可以实现多模态、接触感知的人形机器人恢复，并能泛化到不同扰动幅度、接触几何和动力学偏移。

Abstract: Humanoid robots operating in unstructured environments must recover from unexpected disturbances-a capability that remains challenging for end-to-end control policies. We present RECOVERFORMER, a fully end-to-end humanoid recovery policy that learns when and how to switch among recovery behaviors-including compensatory stepping, hand-environment contact, and center-of-mass reshaping-while maintaining robust performance under model mismatch. The architecture combines a causal transformer over a 50-step observation history with two novel heads: a latent recovery mode that enables smooth transitions among distinct recovery strategies, and a contact affordance head that predicts which environmental surfaces (walls, railings, table edges) are beneficial for stabilization. We evaluate RECOVERFORMER on the Unitree G1 humanoid in MuJoCo. Trained only on open floor, RECOVERFORMER transfers zero shot to walled environments, achieving 100% recovery success across 100-300 N pushes and across wall distances from 0.25-1.4m. Under zero-shot dynamics mismatch, RECOVERFORMER reaches 75.5% at plus +25% mass, 89% under 30 ms latency, 91.5% at low friction, and 99% under compound friction, latency and mass perturbation. The learned latent modes specialize across force regimes without mode-level supervision, validated by t-SNE analysis of 300 episodes. Taken together, these results show that a single end-to-end policy can deliver multi-modal, contact aware humanoid recovery that generalizes across perturbation magnitude, contact geometry, and dynamics shift.

</details>


### [102] [Collaborative Trajectory Prediction via Late Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22973)
*Nadya Abdel Madjid,Murad Mebrahtu,Zakhar Yagudin,Bilal Hassan,Naoufel Werghi,Jorge Dias,Dzmitry Tsetserukou,Majid Khonji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将协作从感知阶段转移到预测模块的晚融合框架，用于车辆间协同轨迹预测。该框架是模型无关的，将协作车辆视为独立异步智能体，通过共享预测结果而非高维特征图来降低通信开销。在OPV2V、V2V4Real和DeepAccident数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比独立预测降低了缺失率并提高了轨迹成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作轨迹预测方法通常在感知阶段融合特征图构建全局场景视图，这导致大量通信开销（交换高维特征表示），且通常假设理想的带宽和同步条件，限制了实际部署。

Method: 提出将协作从感知阶段转移到预测模块的晚融合框架。该框架是模型无关的，将协作车辆视为独立异步智能体，在预测阶段共享预测结果而非原始感知特征。

Result: 在OPV2V、V2V4Real和DeepAccident数据集上，晚融合方法一致降低了缺失率并提高了轨迹成功率（TSR_{0.5}）。在V2V4Real真实数据集上，协作预测相比独立预测分别将两辆智能车的成功率提升了1.69%和1.22%。

Conclusion: 将协作从感知阶段转移到预测模块的晚融合框架，通过共享预测结果而非高维特征图，能有效降低通信开销，同时提升预测性能，更具实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Predicting future trajectories of surrounding traffic agents is critical for safe autonomous navigation and collision avoidance. Despite all advances in the trajectory forecasting realm, the prediction models remains vulnerable to uncertainty caused by occlusions, limited sensing range, and perception errors. Collaborative vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) approaches help reduce this uncertainty by sharing complementary information. Existing collaborative trajectory prediction methods typically fuse feature maps at the perception stage to construct a holistic scene view. Further this holistic representation is decoded into the future trajectories. Such design incurs substantial communication overhead due to the exchange of high-dimensional feature representations and often assumes idealized bandwidth and synchronization, limiting practical deployment. We address these limitations by shifting collaboration from perception to the prediction module and introducing a late-fusion framework for shared forecasts. The framework is model-agnostic and treats collaborating vehicles as independent asynchronous agents. We evaluate the approach on the OPV2V, V2V4Real, and DeepAccident datasets, comparing individual and collaborative forecasting. Across all datasets, late fusion consistently reduces miss rate and improves trajectory success rate ($\mathrm{TSR}_{0.5}$), defined as the fraction of ground-truth agents with final displacement error below 0.5 m. On the real-world V2V4Real dataset, collaborative prediction improves the success rate by $1.69\%$ and $1.22\%$ for both intelligent vehicles, respectively, compared with individual forecasting.

</details>


### [103] [Learning from the Best: Smoothness-Driven Metrics for Data Quality in Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23000)
*Soham Kulkarni,Raayan Dhar,Yuchen Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RINSE框架，利用轨迹平滑度（SAL和TED指标）对演示数据进行质量评分，无需额外策略训练或人工标注，在过滤、检索、重加权三种场景下均能有效提升BC性能，最高可节省5/6数据的同时提升16%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准行为克隆对所有演示数据一视同仁，但现实数据质量参差不齐；现有数据筛选方法需在循环中训练策略或人工标注，可扩展性差。

Method: 提出RINSE框架，使用两种互补指标：SAL（频谱弧长，频域正则性度量）和TED（轨迹-包络距离，接触感知几何偏差空间度量），基于运动控制理论中"平滑度是熟练运动标志"的原理对轨迹评分。

Result: RoboMimic上SAL过滤用1/6数据实现16%成功率提升；真实操作中TED过滤用一半数据实现20%提升；LIBERO-10上STRAP检索重排序提升5.6%；Re-Mix域重加权中与学习分配高度相关(Spearman ρ≥0.89)。

Conclusion: 轨迹平滑度是有效的质量信号，可在过滤、检索、重加权等多种设置下提升BC性能，尤其在噪声大或异质数据场景中效果显著。

Abstract: In behavioral cloning (BC), policy performance is fundamentally limited by demonstration data quality. Real-world datasets contain trajectories of varying quality due to operator skill differences, teleoperation artifacts, and procedural inconsistencies, yet standard BC treats all demonstrations equally. Existing curation methods require costly policy training in the loop or manual annotation, limiting scalability. We propose RINSE (Ranking and INdexing Smooth Examples), a lightweight framework for scoring demonstrations based on trajectory smoothness that is policy-architecture-agnostic and operates on trajectory data alone, with TED additionally using a phase-boundary/contact signal. Grounded in motor control theory, which establishes smoothness as a hallmark of skilled movement, RINSE uses two complementary metrics: Spectral Arc Length (SAL), a spectral measure of frequency-domain regularity, and Trajectory-Envelope Distance (TED), a spatial measure of contact-aware geometric deviation. We show that smoothness filtering can reduce the conditional action variance of the retained data distribution, with downstream effects that can be amplified by action chunking and compounding error. On RoboMimic benchmarks, SAL filtering achieves 16% higher success using one-sixth of the data. On real-world manipulation, TED filtering achieves 20% improvement with half the data. As a retrieval-stage filter within STRAP on LIBERO-10, RINSE re-ranking improves mean success by 5.6%. As soft weights in Re-Mix domain reweighting, RINSE scores produce domain allocations highly correlated with the learned Re-Mix allocations (Spearman $ρ\geq 0.89$). These results support smoothness as a useful quality signal across filtering, retrieval, and reweighting settings, especially in noisy or heterogeneous data regimes.

</details>


### [104] [Vision-Language-Action in Robotics: A Survey of Datasets, Benchmarks, and Data Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23001)
*Ziyao Wang,Bingying Wang,Hanrong Zhang,Tingting Du,Tianyang Chen,Guoheng Sun,Yexiao He,Zheyu Shen,Wanghao Ye,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的数据基础设施进行系统性调研，围绕数据集、基准测试和数据引擎三大支柱展开分析，指出未来VLA的进步将更多依赖于高保真数据引擎与结构化评估协议的协同设计，而非模型架构本身，并提炼出表征对齐、多模态监督、推理评估和可扩展数据生成四大开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLA模型取得了显著进展，但支撑具身学习的数据基础设施这一核心瓶颈仍未得到充分研究。作者认为未来VLA的突破将更多取决于数据引擎与评估协议的协同设计，而非模型架构创新。

Method: 采用以数据为中心的系统性分析方法，围绕三个支柱展开：(1) 数据集：对真实世界与合成语料库按具身多样性、模态构成和动作空间公式进行分类；(2) 基准测试：联合分析任务复杂度和环境结构；(3) 数据引擎：审视基于仿真、视频重建和自动任务生成的范式。

Result: 揭示了数据集中存在的保真度-成本权衡约束、基准测试在组合泛化和长程推理评估方面的结构性缺陷、以及数据引擎在物理基础真实性和仿真到现实迁移方面的共同局限性。

Conclusion: 提出应将数据基础设施作为一等研究问题而非背景关切，以应对表征对齐、多模态监督、推理评估和可扩展数据生成四大挑战。

Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in Vision--Language--Action (VLA) models, a central bottleneck remains underexamined: the data infrastructure that underlies embodied learning. In this survey, we argue that future advances in VLA will depend less on model architecture and more on the co-design of high-fidelity data engines and structured evaluation protocols. To this end, we present a systematic, data-centric analysis of VLA research organized around three pillars: datasets, benchmarks, and data engines. For datasets, we categorize real-world and synthetic corpora along embodiment diversity, modality composition, and action space formulation, revealing a persistent fidelity-cost trade-off that fundamentally constrains large-scale collection. For benchmarks, we analyze task complexity and environment structure jointly, exposing structural gaps in compositional generalization and long-horizon reasoning evaluation that existing protocols fail to address. For data engines, we examine simulation-based, video-reconstruction, and automated task-generation paradigms, identifying their shared limitations in physical grounding and sim-to-real transfer. Synthesizing these analyses, we distill four open challenges: representation alignment, multimodal supervision, reasoning assessment, and scalable data generation. Addressing them, we argue, requires treating data infrastructure as a first-class research problem rather than a background concern.

</details>


### [105] [Equivariant Filter for Radar-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23033)
*Giulio Delama,Jan Michalczyk,Morten Nissov,Martin Scheiber,Alessandro Fornasier,Kostas Alexis,Stephan Weiss*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于等变滤波器（EqF）的雷达-惯性里程计（RIO）方法，利用李群对称性将导航状态与IMU偏置几何耦合，并扩展到雷达-IMU外参标定和多状态约束更新。该方法在保持一致性和鲁棒性方面优于传统EKF-RIO，即使在完全错误的外参初始化下也能有效收敛。在两种UAV上的实验证明了其SOTA精度和更强的收敛能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）的雷达-惯性里程计（RIO）高度依赖雷达与IMU之间的精确外参标定，且对大线性化误差敏感，容易性能下降甚至发散。为克服这些局限性，本文提出基于等变滤波器（EqF）的RIO方法。

Method: 提出了基于李群对称性的等变滤波器（EqF）用于RIO，将导航状态与IMU偏置进行几何耦合，并扩展了雷达-IMU外参标定和多状态约束更新（MSC）模块。等变公式天然保持一致性并增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在两种不同无人机（UAV）上的真实世界实验表明，所提出的EqF-RIO在外参标定正确时达到SOTA精度，在较大标定误差下相比传统EKF-RIO具有更优的收敛性能。

Conclusion: EqF-RIO通过李群等变设计，有效解决了EKF-RIO对外参标定误差敏感的问题，在保持高精度的同时大幅提升了系统的鲁棒性和收敛能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Radar-Inertial Odometry (RIO) based on the Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) relies on accurate extrinsic calibration between the radar and the Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) and is sensitive to disturbances, as large linearization errors can degrade performance or even cause divergence. To address these limitations, this letter proposes an Equivariant Filter (EqF) for RIO based on a Lie group symmetry that geometrically couples navigation states and IMU biases, extending it to incorporate radar-IMU extrinsic calibration and multi-state constraint updates. This equivariant formulation inherently preserves consistency and enhances robustness, enabling reliable state estimation even under poor or completely wrong initialization of calibration states. Real-world experiments on two different Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) show that the proposed EqF-RIO achieves state-of-the-art accuracy under correct extrinsic calibration and offers improved convergence under large calibration errors, where the conventional EKF-RIO fails. Evaluation code is open-sourced.

</details>


### [106] [Control Barrier Functions Solved with Hierarchical Quadratic Programming for Safe Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23039)
*Rui Luo,Jonas Mariager Jakobsen,Wesley Roozing,Federico Califano,Cheng Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于控制障碍函数（CBF）的分层二次规划（HQP）框架，用于物理人机交互场景，通过在层次结构中灵活设计性能任务和安全任务，平衡安全性和性能，并在真实冗余机器人上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理人机交互中安全至关重要，现有CBF方法在任务冲突时难以保证可行性，需要一种更灵活的方式来平衡安全与性能。

Method: 提出CBF-based Hierarchical Quadratic Programming (HQP)框架，将CBF约束融入分层二次规划中，允许在不同层级灵活安排性能任务和安全任务，通过层次化松弛解决任务冲突问题。

Result: 在真实冗余机器人上进行了大量实验，验证了该方法在有效性、灵活性和通用性方面的表现。

Conclusion: CBF-based HQP框架能够更灵活地平衡物理人机交互中的安全与性能，为多任务冲突场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Physical human-robot interaction offers the potential to leverage human intelligence and robot physical capabilities to enable a range of exciting applications, e.g., collaborative robots for rehabilitation. Safety is critical for the successful deployment of this kind of robotic system. In recent years, Control Barrier Function (CBF) has emerged as an effective approach to enforce safety guarantees, which has been widely applied in various applications, from adaptive cruise control to navigation of legged robots. CBFs can be solved in a Quadratic Programming (QP) problem, which can include many CBF-formulated tasks. To manage a large number of safety tasks, a hierarchical CBF has been used to allow hierarchical relaxation of safety tasks to ensure the feasibility of a solution in the presence of conflicting tasks. In this work, we propose to use a CBF-based Hierarchical Quadratic Programming (HQP) framework in physical human-robot interaction to allow us to design both performance tasks (e.g., preserve the desired behavior at the human-robot interaction point) and safety tasks at any level of a hierarchy to balance the safety and the performance in a more flexible way. Extensive experiments were carried out on a real redundant robot to validate the effectiveness, flexibility, and generality of this approach.

</details>


### [107] [A Lightweight Toggleable Adhesion Prototype for Multirotor UAV Landing on Tilting Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23074)
*Teighin Nordholt,Melissa Greeff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级可切换吸附机构，通过电机驱动的螺旋钩与钩环材料配合，使多旋翼无人机能在倾斜高达43度的无人水面舰船上实现80%的着陆成功率（基线为40%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼无人机在无人水面舰船上的自主着陆因波浪引起的倾斜、风扰和着陆区域受限而极具挑战，现有方法姿态容忍度小，难以实现可靠着陆。

Method: 设计了一种电机驱动螺旋钩机构，与着陆表面的钩环材料啮合，实现着陆时的主动吸附和起飞时的可控释放；采用改进的Crazyflie 2.0和定制倾斜平台在固定角度下进行验证，仅使用简单的垂直PID控制器。

Result: 在高达43度的平台倾斜下，使用适当驱动设置，着陆成功率从基线的40%提升至80%。

Conclusion: 所提出的轻量级可切换吸附机构能显著提高多旋翼无人机在倾斜平台上的着陆可靠性，为在恶劣海况下实现持久海上作业提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Autonomous multirotor landings on uncrewed surface vessels (USVs) are critical for persistent maritime operations but remain challenging due to wave-induced tilt, wind disturbances, and limited landing area. Many existing approaches exhibit small pose tolerance for reliable landing. This paper presents a lightweight toggleable adhesion mechanism to improve landing reliability. The system uses a motor-driven corkscrew that engages hook-and-loop material on the landing surface, enabling active adhesion during landing and controlled release during takeoff. We evaluate a prototype using a modified Crazyflie 2.0 and a custom tilting platform at fixed angles representative of extreme wave conditions. Using only a simple vertical PID controller, the proposed approach increases landing success from an average of 40% (baseline) to 80% across platform tilts up to 43 degrees using appropriately selected actuation settings.

</details>


### [108] [Breaking Lock-In: Preserving Steerability under Low-Data VLA Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23121)
*Suning Huang,Jiaqi Shao,Ke Wang,Qianzhong Chen,Jiankai Sun,Yanjiang Guo,Mac Schwager,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeLock通过保留视觉基础和应用测试时对比提示引导，解决VLA策略在低数据微调后出现的概念锁定和空间锁定问题，无需额外监督信号或增强数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉-语言-动作策略在少量演示数据集上后训练后，会停止响应新指令，仅局限于后训练中观察到的行为（锁定现象），表现为概念锁定（固着于训练对象/属性）和空间锁定（固着于训练空间目标）。现有解决方法需要额外监督信号或增强数据集。

Method: DeLock方法：1) 在后训练期间保留视觉基础能力；2) 在测试时应用对比提示引导，根据新指令调整策略的去噪动力学。

Result: 在8项仿真和真实世界评估中，DeLock持续优于强基线，性能匹配或超过使用更多精选演示后训练的最先进通用策略。

Conclusion: 策略内部的预训练知识足够解决锁定问题，无需额外监督信号或增强数据集。

Abstract: Have you ever post-trained a generalist vision-language-action (VLA) policy on a small demonstration dataset, only to find that it stops responding to new instructions and is limited to behaviors observed during post-training? We identify this phenomenon as lock-in: after low-data, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), the policy becomes overly specialized to the post-training data and fails to generalize to novel instructions, manifesting as concept lock-in (fixation on training objects/attributes) and spatial lock-in (fixation on training spatial targets). Many existing remedies introduce additional supervision signals, such as those derived from foundation models or auxiliary objectives, or rely on augmented datasets to recover generalization. In this paper, we show that the policy's internal pre-trained knowledge is sufficient: DeLock mitigates lock-in by preserving visual grounding during post-training and applying test-time contrastive prompt guidance to steer the policy's denoising dynamics according to novel instructions. Across eight simulation and real-world evaluations, DeLock consistently outperforms strong baselines and matches or exceeds the performance of a state-of-the-art generalist policy post-trained with substantially more curated demonstrations.

</details>


### [109] [Cooperative Informative Sensing for Monitoring Dynamic Indoor Environments via Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23179)
*Kanghoon Lee,Matthew M. Sato,Jinnyeong Yang,Seungro Lee,Sujin Lee,Jiachen Li,Kuk-Jin Yoon,Jinkyoo Park,Kincho H. Law,Yoonjin Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多智能体强化学习的框架，使多机器人团队通过分散控制直接优化对人类活动的监测精度，在部分可观测环境下显著优于传统覆盖、持续监测和无学习基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内环境中的多机器人监测任务需要高精度的人体活动监测，但现有方法依赖覆盖或访问频率等目标，与监测准确性的需求关联较弱。

Method: 将协同主动观测形式化为分散控制问题，使用多智能体强化学习（MARL）学习协作策略，架构支持可变人数和时间依赖关系。

Result: 在多种室内环境和监测任务中，所提方法一致优于经典覆盖、持续监测和无学习基线，并对观测人数变化具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于MARL的分散协同主动观测能有效提升多机器人监测精度，为实际部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Monitoring human activity in indoor environments is important for applications such as facility management, safety assessment, and space utilization analysis. While mobile robot teams offer the potential to actively improve observation quality, existing multi-robot monitoring and active perception approaches typically rely on coverage or visitation based objectives that are weakly aligned with the accuracy requirements of human-centric monitoring tasks. In this work, we formulate cooperative active observation as a decentralized control problem in which multiple robots adjust their motion to directly optimize monitoring accuracy under partial observability. We propose a learning-based framework for cooperative policies from decentralized observations using multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), supported by an architecture that handles variable numbers of humans and temporal dependencies. Simulation results across diverse indoor environments and monitoring tasks show that the proposed approach consistently outperforms classical coverage, persistent monitoring, and learning-free multi-robot baselines, while remaining robust to changes in the number of observed humans.

</details>


### [110] [BridgeACT: Bridging Human Demonstrations to Robot Actions via Unified Tool-Target Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23249)
*Yifan Han,Jianxiang Liu,Haoyu Zhang,Yuqi Gu,Yunhan Guo,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BridgeACT是一个从人类视频直接学习机器人操作的框架，无需任何机器人演示数据，通过将操作分解为"抓取位置"和"移动方式"两个互补问题，利用可负担性作为中间表征实现从人类演示到机器人动作的迁移，在真实世界任务中优于基线方法并泛化到未见过的物体、场景和视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类视频中学习机器人操作具有数据规模和多样性的优势，但将人类演示迁移到可执行的机器人行为仍然具有挑战性。现有方法要么依赖机器人数据进行下游适配，要么学习的可负担性表征停留在感知层面，无法直接支持真实世界的执行。

Method: BridgeACT提出将可负担性作为与具体实体无关的中间表征，连接人类演示和机器人动作。具体将操作分解为两个互补问题：(1) 哪里抓取——在当前场景中定位任务相关的可负担性区域；(2) 如何移动——从人类演示中预测任务条件的3D运动可负担性。通过抓取模块和轻量级闭环运动控制器将可负担性映射到机器人动作。复杂操作任务被表示为可负担性操作的组合。

Result: 在真实世界操作任务上，BridgeACT优于先前基线方法，并能泛化到未见过的物体、场景和视角。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了仅从人类视频学习无需机器人演示数据即可实现真实机器人操作是可行的，且具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning robot manipulation from human videos is appealing due to the scale and diversity of human demonstrations, but transferring such demonstrations to executable robot behavior remains challenging. Prior work either relies on robot data for downstream adaptation or learns affordance representations that remain at the perception level and do not directly support real-world execution. We present BridgeACT, an affordance-driven framework that learns robotic manipulation directly from human videos without requiring any robot demonstration data. Our key idea is to model affordance as an embodiment-agnostic intermediate representation that bridges human demonstrations and robot actions. BridgeACT decomposes manipulation into two complementary problems: where to grasp and how to move. To this end, BridgeACT first grounds task-relevant affordance regions in the current scene, and then predicts task-conditioned 3D motion affordances from human demonstrations. The resulting affordances are mapped to robot actions through a grasping module and a lightweight closed-loop motion controller, enabling direct deployment on real robots. In addition, we represent complex manipulation tasks as compositions of affordance operations, which allows a unified treatment of diverse tasks and object-to-object interactions. Experiments on real-world manipulation tasks show that BridgeACT outperforms prior baselines and generalizes to unseen objects, scenes, and viewpoints.

</details>


### [111] [Modular Sensory Stream for Integrating Physical Feedback in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23272)
*Jimin Lee,Huiwon Jang,Myungkyu Koo,Jungwoo Park,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoSS是一个模块化感官流框架，通过解耦的多模态流和两阶段训练策略，使视觉-语言-动作模型能够整合多种物理信号（如触觉和力矩）进行动作预测，在真实世界实验中取得了协同性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型（VLAs）在融入物理感知信号时通常只关注单一模态，未能捕捉真实世界交互中多种物理信号的异构性和互补性。

Method: 提出MoSS框架：1）解耦的多模态流通过联合跨模态自注意力将异构物理信号集成到动作流中；2）两阶段训练方案，早期冻结预训练VLA参数以稳定融入新模态；3）辅助任务预测未来物理信号以捕捉接触交互动态。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，MoSS成功将多种物理信号（触觉和力矩）融入VLAs，实现多个信号的协同性能增益。

Conclusion: MoSS通过模块化设计有效扩展了VLAs的多模态感知能力，验证了异构物理信号融合对于提升动作预测性能的有效性。

Abstract: Humans understand and interact with the real world by relying on diverse physical feedback beyond visual perception. Motivated by this, recent approaches attempt to incorporate physical sensory signals into Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs). However, they typically focus on a single type of physical signal, failing to capture the heterogeneous and complementary nature of real-world interactions. In this paper, we propose MoSS, a modular sensory stream framework that adapts VLAs to leverage multiple sensory signals for action prediction. Specifically, we introduce decoupled modality streams that integrate heterogeneous physical signals into the action stream via joint cross-modal self-attention. To enable stable incorporation of new modalities, we adopt a two-stage training scheme that freezes pretrained VLA parameters in the early stage. Furthermore, to better capture contact interaction dynamics, we incorporate an auxiliary task that predicts future physical signals. Through extensive real-world experiments, we demonstrate that MoSS successfully augments VLAs to leverage diverse physical signals (i.e., tactile and torque), integrating multiple signals to achieve synergistic performance gains.

</details>


### [112] [An Efficient Beam Search Algorithm for Active Perception in Mobile Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23327)
*Kaixian Qu,Han Wang,Victor Klemm,Cesar Cadena,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出节点束搜索算法(NBS)和快速探索随机环图(RRAG)，用于解决自主机器人主动感知中的路径规划问题，在多个任务上性能优于现有方法至少20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动感知方法要么求解计算昂贵的旅行商问题，要么采用效率低但过度约束的最短路径树方法，缺乏可扩展且高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出了三个贡献：(1) 节点级束搜索算法(NBS)，每节点保留Top-B候选路径以更好地探索解空间；(2) 引入"前沿"概念和期望增益指标以平衡探索与利用；(3) 快速探索随机环图(RRAG)，保留全方向采样并通过局部回退规划器确保复杂环境中的连通性。

Result: 在三个代表性主动感知任务中，NBS结合RRAG均取得最高性能，在至少一个任务上优于最先进算法20%以上，并在真实机器人平台上得到验证。

Conclusion: NBS算法与RRAG图构建方法的组合有效解决了主动感知中的可扩展性与解质量权衡问题，为自主机器人主动感知提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Active perception is a fundamental problem in autonomous robotics in which the robot must decide where to move and what to sense in order to obtain the most informative observations for accomplishing its mission. Existing approaches either solve a computationally expensive traveling salesman problem over heuristically selected informative nodes, or adopt a more efficient but overly constrained shortest path tree formulation. To address these limitations, we explore beam search algorithms as scalable alternatives. While the standard beam search provides scalability by preserving the top-B paths at each depth level, it is prone to local optima and exhibits parameter sensitivity. Our first contribution is a node-wise beam search (NBS) algorithm, which maintains top-B candidates per node to enable more effective exploration of the solution space. Systematic benchmarking on graphs shows that NBS consistently outperforms other baselines and maintains strong performance even at low beam widths. As a second contribution, we integrate the concept of frontiers into the path selection criterion, introducing the expected gain metric, which better balances exploration and exploitation compared to existing alternatives. Our third contribution proposes the rapidly-exploring random annulus graph (RRAG), a novel graph construction method that preserves full orientation sampling and ensures connectivity in cluttered environments through a fallback local sampling-based planner. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NBS combined with RRAG achieves the highest performance across all three representative active perception tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art algorithms by at least 20% in one or more tasks. We further validate the approach on real robotic platforms in different scenarios.

</details>


### [113] [Learning from Demonstration with Failure Awareness for Safe Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23360)
*Xianghui Wang,Siwei Cheng,Shanze Wang,Xinming Zhang,Dan Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种故障感知学习框架，通过解耦成功与失败数据的作用——用失败数据 shaping 危险区域的价值估计，而策略学习仅使用成功演示——在离线强化学习设置下有效利用碰撞等失败经验提升机器人导航安全性，仿真与实物实验均证明该方法能降低碰撞率同时保持任务成功率，并具有良好的跨环境和跨平台泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的从演示学习（LfD）方法在机器人导航中广泛使用，但其演示数据主要由成功行为构成，对不安全状态的覆盖严重不足。当机器人遇到分布外场景时，安全性很差。而碰撞等失败经验包含关于危险区域的关键信息，却未被充分利用，因为失败数据无法为动作模仿提供有效指导，简单纳入反而会降低策略性能。

Method: 提出一个故障感知学习框架，明确解耦成功数据与失败数据的作用：失败数据用于 shaping 危险区域的价值估计，策略学习则仅限于成功演示。该方法在离线强化学习框架下实现，使失败数据得以有效利用而不破坏策略行为。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界环境中的实验表明，该框架能够持续降低碰撞率，同时保持任务成功率，并在不同环境和机器人平台上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该故障感知学习框架通过解耦成功与失败数据的作用，成功解决了利用失败经验提升导航安全性的难题，为机器人学习中的安全关键问题提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration is widely used for robot navigation, yet it suffers from a fundamental limitation: demonstrations consist predominantly of successful behaviors and provide limited coverage of unsafe states. This limitation leads to poor safety when the robot encounters scenarios beyond the demonstration distribution. Failure experiences, such as collisions, contain essential information about unsafe regions, but remain underutilized. The key difficulty lies in the fact that failure data do not provide valid guidance for action imitation, and their naive incorporation into policy learning often degrades performance. We address this challenge by proposing a failure-aware learning framework that explicitly decouples the roles of success and failure data. In this framework, failure experiences are used to shape value estimation in hazardous regions, while policy learning is restricted to successful demonstrations. This separation enables the effective use of failure data without corrupting policy behavior. We implement this design within an offline reinforcement learning (RL) setting and evaluate it in both simulation and real-world environments. The results show that our framework consistently reduces collision rates while preserving the task success rate, and demonstrate strong generalization across different environments and robot platforms.

</details>


### [114] [EgoLive: A Large-Scale Egocentric Dataset from Real-World Human Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23570)
*Yihang Li,Xuelong Wei,Jingzhou Luo,Yingjing Xiao,Yibo Bai,Guangyuan Zhou,Teng Zou,Chenguang Gui,Jiajun Wen,He Zhang,Kangliang Chen,Xing Pan,Shuaiyan Liu,Daming Wang,Tao An,Jiayi Li,Shibo Jin,Wanwan Zhang,Tianyu Wang,Boren Wei,Zhixuan Huang,Fangsheng Liu,Ruodai Li,Hui Zhang,Anson Li,Yicheng Gong,Peng Cao,Jiaming Liang,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoLive是一个大规模、高质量、专为机器人操作学习设计的开放源代码第一人称视角数据集，包含家庭服务、零售等真实场景的多模态标注数据，旨在解决机器人学习领域数据集规模小、质量低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习数据集主要依赖遥操作和通用操作接口等方法收集，存在可扩展性差和难以在真实世界部署的局限性。人类第一人称视频收集作为一种可扩展、自然且无约束的数据收集方式，为解决这一问题提供了新途径。

Method: 通过定制头戴式捕捉设备采集真实世界无约束场景中的任务导向人类日常活动数据，提供高精度多模态标注，覆盖家庭服务、零售等垂直工作领域。

Result: EgoLive是当前最大的开源标注第一人称数据集，专注真实世界任务导向的人类日常活动，数据质量领先，包含多样化的真实场景数据。

Conclusion: EgoLive通过提供一个可扩展的高质量数据集，有望加速可泛化机器人模型的突破，促进机器人系统的真实部署。

Abstract: The advancement of robot learning is currently hindered by the scarcity of large-scale, high-quality datasets. While established data collection methods such as teleoperation and universal manipulation interfaces dominate current datasets, they suffer from inherent limitations in scalability and real-world deployability. Human egocentric video collection, by contrast, has emerged as a promising approach to enable scalable, natural and in-the-wild data collection. As such, we present EgoLive, a large-scale, high-quality egocentric dataset designed explicitly for robot manipulation learning. EgoLive establishes three distinctive technical advantages over existing egocentric datasets: first, it represents the largest open-source annotated egocentric dataset focused on real-world task-oriented human routines to date; second, it delivers leading data quality via a customized head-mounted capture device and comprehensive high-precision multi-modal annotations; third, all data is collected exclusively in unconstrained real-world scenarios and encompasses vertical field human working data, including home service, retail, and other practical work scenarios, providing superior diversity and ecological validity. With the introduction of EgoLive, we aim to provide the research community with a scalable, high-quality dataset that accelerates breakthroughs in generalizable robotic models and facilitates the real-world deployment of robot systems.

</details>


### [115] [Tube Diffusion Policy: Reactive Visual-Tactile Policy Learning for Contact-rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23609)
*Teng Xue,Alberto Rigo,Bingjian Huang,Jiayi Shen,Zhengtong Xu,Nick Colonnese,Amirhossein H. Memar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Tube Diffusion Policy (TDP)，一种融合扩散模型模仿学习与管状反馈控制的新型反应式视觉-触觉策略学习框架。TDP通过在名义动作块周围学习观测条件化的反馈流，形成"动作管"，实现接触丰富操作中的快速自适应反应。在Push-T基准和三项视觉-触觉灵巧操作任务中，TDP一致优于现有最先进的模仿学习基线，并在两个真实世界实验中验证了其在接触不确定性和外部干扰下的鲁棒反应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于动作块（action chunking）的模仿学习方法在接触丰富操作中存在根本性局限——无法在执行过程中对未预见的观测做出反应。接触丰富场景中的物理不确定性和高频触觉反馈迫切需要快速、反应式的控制，而现有方法无法满足这一需求。

Method: 提出Tube Diffusion Policy (TDP)，将扩散模型驱动的模仿学习与管状反馈控制相结合。核心创新在于学习一个以观测为条件的反馈流，将其叠加在名义动作块周围，形成"动作管"（action tube），从而在保持扩散模型表达能力的同时，实现执行过程中的快速自适应反馈调整。此外，该动作管支持逐步修正机制，可显著减少扩散模型所需的去噪步数。

Result: 在Push-T基准和三个额外的视觉-触觉灵巧操作任务上，TDP一致优于现有最先进的模仿学习基线。两个真实世界实验验证了其在接触不确定性和外部干扰下的鲁棒反应能力。动作管的逐步修正机制显著减少了所需的去噪步数，适合实时高频反馈控制。

Conclusion: TDP有效弥合了基于扩散模型的模仿学习与反馈控制之间的鸿沟，为接触丰富操作中的实时、高频反应式控制提供了可行的解决方案，兼具生成模型的表达能力和反馈控制的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation is central to many everyday human activities, requiring continuous adaptation to contact uncertainty and external disturbances through multi-modal perception, particularly vision and tactile feedback. While imitation learning has shown strong potential for learning complex manipulation behaviors, most existing approaches rely on action chunking, which fundamentally limits their ability to react to unforeseen observations during execution. This limitation becomes especially critical in contact-rich scenarios, where physical uncertainty and high-frequency tactile feedback demand rapid, reactive control. To address this challenge, we propose Tube Diffusion Policy (TDP), a novel reactive visual-tactile policy learning framework that bridges diffusion-based imitation learning with tube-based feedback control. By leveraging the expressive power of generative models, TDP learns an observation-conditioned feedback flow around nominal action chunks, forming an action tube that enables fast and adaptive reactions during execution. We evaluate TDP on the widely used Push-T benchmark and three additional challenging visual-tactile dexterous manipulation tasks. Across all benchmarks, TDP consistently outperforms state-of-the-art imitation learning baselines. Two real-world experiments further validate its robust reactivity under contact uncertainty and external disturbances. Moreover, the step-wise correction mechanism enabled by action tube significantly reduces the required denoising steps, making TDP well suited for real-time, high-frequency feedback control in contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [116] [Move-Then-Operate: Behavioral Phasing for Human-Like Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23620)
*Haoming Xu,Lei Lei,Jie Gu,Chu Tang,Jingmin Chen,Ruiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Move-Then-Operate框架，将机器人操作显式解耦为粗放移动（Move）和接触关键操作（Operate）两个阶段，通过双专家策略路由架构实现，在RoboTwin2基准上平均成功率68.9%，比单一体基线π₀提升24%，仅需40%的训练步数即可达到峰值性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单一体策略将移动和操作两种异质行为混为一体，缺乏对阶段特定动力学的结构归纳偏置，导致高精度操作任务效率低下。

Method: 提出Move-Then-Operate框架，显式解耦为粗放重定位（Move）和接触关键交互（Operate）两个阶段；架构采用双专家策略（dual-expert policy）并由可学习的阶段选择器（phase selector）路由；阶段标签通过基于MLLM的管线自动生成，以末端执行器速度和子任务分解等轻量级上下文线索为条件。

Result: 在RoboTwin2基准上平均成功率68.9%，比单一体基线π₀提升24%，匹配或超越使用10倍数据训练的模型，且仅需40%的训练步数即可达到峰值性能。

Conclusion: 移动和操作阶段的架构解耦是一种高效且有效的策略，能够掌握高精度机器人操作任务。

Abstract: We present Move-Then-Operate, a Vision language action framework that explicitly decouples robotic manipulation into two distinct behavioral phases: coarse relocation (move) and contact-critical interaction (operate). Unlike monolithic policies that conflate these heterogeneous regimes, our architecture employs a dual-expert policy routed by a learnable phase selector, introducing a structural inductive bias that isolates phase-specific dynamics. Phase labels are automatically generated via an MLLM-based pipeline conditioned on lightweight contextual cues such as end-effector velocity and subtask decomposition to ensure alignment with human motor patterns. Evaluated on the RoboTwin2 benchmark, our method achieves an average success rate of $68.9\%$, outperforming the monolithic $π_0$ baseline by $24\%$. It matches or exceeds models trained on $10\times$ more data and reaches peak performance in $40\%$ fewer training steps, demonstrating that architectural disentanglement of move and operate phases is a highly effective and efficient strategy for mastering high-precision manipulation.

</details>


### [117] [Safe Navigation in Unknown and Cluttered Environments via Direction-Aware Convex Free-Region Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23648)
*Zhicheng Song,Yongjian Li,Kai Chen,Yulin Li,Fan Shi,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种导航框架，将候选运动方向和机器人几何形状联合融入凸自由区域生成，并通过连续安全轨迹生成实现无碰撞导航，在2D/3D及真实机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有凸自由区域生成方法仅根据周围障碍物几何扩大区域，在杂乱环境中无法同时兼顾机器人几何形状和候选运动方向的可通行延伸，导致窄通道场景下导航受限；同时，离散化轨迹样本的安全检查无法保证连续无碰撞运动。

Method: 提出FRGraph框架：1）联合候选运动方向和机器人几何生成凸自由区域；2）区域内进行几何感知的目标位姿选择与轨迹生成；3）基于Lipschitz条件的连续安全认证与局部优化；4）基于区域图维护并支持增量规划。

Result: 在杂乱2D导航场景中，生成的自由区域更符合下游通行需求，实现可靠无碰撞导航；在四足机器人和无人机上的3D及真实场景实验验证了框架的可扩展性和实际适用性。

Conclusion: 通过将运动方向和机器人几何显式融入凸区域生成，并引入连续安全认证机制，有效解决了杂乱环境中窄通道导航与连续碰撞安全性两大难题。

Abstract: Convex free regions provide a structured and optimization-friendly representation of collision-free space for robot navigation in unknown and cluttered environments. However, existing methods typically enlarge local collision-free regions mainly according to surrounding obstacle geometry. In cluttered environments, such strategies may fail to generate regions that both accommodate robot geometry and preserve traversable extension along candidate motion directions, thereby limiting downstream traversal, especially in narrow passages. Even when such a region is available, safe motion generation remains challenging, because safety checking at discretized trajectory samples does not guarantee continuously collision-free motion when robot geometry is modeled explicitly. To address these issues, we propose a navigation framework that jointly incorporates candidate motion directions and robot geometry into convex free-region generation, and achieves continuously collision-free motion through continuous-safe trajectory generation. Within each region, the framework performs geometry-aware target pose selection and trajectory generation, together with Lipschitz-based continuous safety certification and local refinement. The resulting free regions and candidate motions are maintained in a region-based graph to support incremental planning. Quantitative results in cluttered 2D navigation scenarios show that the proposed method generates free regions better aligned with downstream traversal and enables reliable collision-free navigation, while additional 3D and real-world experiments on a quadrupedal robot and a UAV demonstrate the extensibility and practical applicability of the framework. The open-source project can be found at https://github.com/ZhichengSong6/FRGraph.

</details>


### [118] [Decentralized Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Collaborative Exploration for Indoor and Outdoor 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23693)
*Yuxiang Li,Kun Chen,Jiancheng Wang,Shihao Fang,Haoyao Chen,Yunhui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向异构多机器人系统的去中心化协作框架，通过改进的超体素分割、任务视图聚类与HMDMTSP建模，结合改进遗传算法求解，实现了室内外3D环境的高效自主探索，在探索效率和通信开销上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构多机器人系统在复杂环境中适应性强，但如何充分发挥各机器人潜力、实现高效协同探索仍是一个核心挑战。

Method: (1) 设计了融合地形与观测指标的基础感知地图，并改进超体素分割简化地图结构，形成支持轻量化通信的高层表示；(2) 对异构机器人的遍历与观测能力建模，从不完整超体素中提取任务视图并按需求聚类；(3) 将视图-聚类分配建模为异构多车场多旅行商问题(HMDMTSP)，引入约束条件；设计改进遗传算法求解；(4) 消除聚类内冗余视图以优化探索路径，并解决机器人间的运动路径冲突。

Result: 在杂乱室内外环境中的仿真与实地实验表明，该方法能有效协调异构机器人间的探索任务，在探索效率和通信节省方面均优于现有先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的去中心化协作框架能够有效解决异构多机器人系统在3D环境自主探索中的协同问题，兼顾了探索效率与通信开销的优化。

Abstract: Heterogeneous multi-robot systems feature significant adaptability for complex environments. However, effective collaboration that fully exploits the robots' potential remains a core challenge. This paper proposes a decentralized collaborative framework for heterogeneous multi-robot systems to autonomously explore indoor and outdoor 3D environments. First, a basic perception map that integrates terrain and observation metrics is designed. Improved supervoxel segmentation is developed to simplify the map structure and form a high-level representation that supports lightweight communication. Second, the traversal and observation capabilities of heterogeneous robots are modeled to evaluate the requirements of task views derived from incomplete supervoxels. These task views are grouped by requirements and clustered to streamline assignment. Subsequently, the view-cluster assignment is formulated as a heterogeneous multi-depot multi-traveling salesman problem (HMDMTSP) that incorporates constraints between view-cluster requirements and robot capabilities. An improved genetic algorithm is developed to efficiently solve this problem while ensuring global consistency. Based on the assignments, redundant views within clusters are eliminated to refine exploration routes. Finally, conflicts between robots' motion paths are resolved. Simulations and field experiments in cluttered indoor and outdoor environments demonstrate that our approach effectively coordinates exploration tasks among heterogeneous robots, achieving superior exploration efficiency and communication savings compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [119] [Real-Time Non-Contact Force Compensation for Wrist-Mounted Force/Torque Sensors in Haptic-Enabled Robotic Surgery Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23696)
*Walid Shaker,Mustafa Suphi Erden*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人手术中力触觉反馈成本高的问题，在低成本训练平台RoboScope上集成腕装力/力矩传感器，并提出基于递归最小二乘法的实时补偿方法，实现了非接触力/力矩误差降低95%和91%以上，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助手术中力触觉反馈长期缺失，商用系统虽已集成但成本高昂，限制了训练和研究中的可及性。作者旨在填补低成本方案中的力觉反馈空白。

Method: 在已有低成本手术训练平台RoboScope上增加腕装F/T传感器，并提出基于递归最小二乘法（RLS）的实时补偿方法，自动处理重力、传感器偏置、安装偏移等非接触力/力矩干扰，无需数据集采集和频繁重新校准。

Result: 非接触力补偿误差降低超过95%，非接触力矩补偿误差降低超过91%，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够为机器人手术训练和研究提供可靠的低成本力触觉反馈方案。

Abstract: Haptic feedback has been a long-missed feature in robotic-assisted surgery, one that would allow surgeons to perceive tissue properties and apply controlled forces during delicate procedures. Although commercial robotic systems have begun to integrate haptic technologies, their high costs limit accessibility for training and research purposes. To address this gap, we extend our previously developed low-cost robotic surgery training setup, RoboScope, by incorporating a wrist-mounted force/torque (F/T) sensor for haptic feedback training. Wrist-mounted sensing avoids many challenges associated with tip-mounted sensors but introduces additional non-contact forces, such as gravity, sensor bias, installation offsets, and associated torques, which compromise measurement accuracy. In this paper, we propose a robust real-time compensation method based on recursive least squares (RLS). This method eliminates the need for dataset collection and frequent recalibration while adapting to changing operating conditions. Experimental validation demonstrates that the proposed approach achieves over 95% error reduction in non-contact force compensation and more than 91% in non-contact torque compensation, significantly outperforming existing methods. These results highlight the potential of our approach for providing reliable haptic feedback in robotic surgery training and research.

</details>


### [120] [Unleashing the Agility of Wheeled-Legged Robots for High-Dynamic Reflexive Obstacle Evasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23761)
*Yongen Zhao,Zihao Xu,Wenzhi Lu,Zhen Chu,Ce Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AWARE框架，一种用于轮腿机器人的分层强化学习避障方法，在仿真和实物平台上实现了高动态障碍物规避和多种涌现式逃避行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮腿机器人兼具轮式能效与腿部地形适应性，但在面对快速移动障碍物时，由于混合形态、模态耦合和非完整约束，难以实现高动态反射式规避。

Method: 提出AWARE（Adaptive Wheeled-Legged Avoidance and Reflexive Evasion），一种分层强化学习框架，使轮腿机器人能够自主习得前冲、侧闪等多种涌现式逃避步态。

Result: 在Isaac Lab仿真和M20实物平台上，AWARE在多种动态场景下实现了鲁棒且敏捷的障碍物规避，并展现出行为上不同的逃避策略。

Conclusion: AWARE框架有效发挥了轮腿机器人的混合形态优势，实现了高动态反射式避障能力，证明了轮腿机器人的内在反射敏捷性。

Abstract: Wheeled-legged robots combine the energy efficiency of wheeled locomotion with the terrain adaptability of legged systems, making them promising platforms for agile mobility in complex and dynamic environments. However, enabling high-dynamic reflexive evasion against fast-moving obstacles remains challenging due to the hybrid morphology, mode coupling, and non-holonomic constraints of such platforms. In this work, we propose AWARE, Adaptive Wheeled-Legged Avoidance and Reflexive Evasion, a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework for high-dynamic obstacle avoidance in wheeled-legged robots. The proposed system naturally exhibits diverse emergent gaits and evasive behaviors, including forward lunge and lateral dodge, thereby leveraging the robot's hybrid morphology to enhance agility under highly dynamic threats. Extensive experiments in Isaac Lab simulation and real-world deployment on the M20 platform across diverse dynamic scenarios demonstrate that AWARE achieves robust and agile obstacle avoidance while revealing behaviorally distinct evasive strategies. These results highlight both the practical effectiveness of AWARE and the intrinsic reflexive agility of wheeled-legged robots.

</details>


### [121] [Vision-Language-Action Safety: Threats, Challenges, Evaluations, and Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23775)
*Qi Li,Bo Yin,Weiqi Huang,Ruhao Liu,Bojun Zou,Runpeng Yu,Jingwen Ye,Weihao Yu,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的安全性问题进行了统一综述，沿攻击时机和防御时机两条时间轴组织文献，涵盖攻击、防御、评估与部署四个维度，并指出了该领域的五大待解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型作为具身智能的统一范式正在兴起，但其具身特性带来了物理后果不可逆、多模态攻击面广、实时延迟约束严格、长程轨迹误差传播及数据供应链脆弱等全新安全挑战，而现有文献分散在多个独立领域，缺乏统一视角。

Method: 沿两条并行时间轴（攻击时机：训练时vs.推理时；防御时机：训练时vs.推理时）组织文献，通过四个透镜（攻击、防御、评估、部署）系统梳理现有研究。

Result: 系统梳理了训练时威胁（数据投毒、后门攻击）和推理时攻击（对抗补丁、跨模态扰动、语义越狱、冻结攻击），综述了训练时和运行时防御方法，分析了现有基准与评估指标，并讨论了六大部署场景的安全挑战。

Conclusion: VLA安全面临五大开放问题：具身轨迹的可认证鲁棒性、物理可实现防御、安全感知训练、统一运行时安全架构、以及标准化评估。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are emerging as a unified substrate for embodied intelligence. This shift raises a new class of safety challenges, stemming from the embodied nature of VLA systems, including irreversible physical consequences, a multimodal attack surface across vision, language, and state, real-time latency constraints on defense, error propagation over long-horizon trajectories, and vulnerabilities in the data supply chain. Yet the literature remains fragmented across robotic learning, adversarial machine learning, AI alignment, and autonomous systems safety. This survey provides a unified and up-to-date overview of safety in Vision-Language-Action models. We organize the field along two parallel timing axes, attack timing (training-time vs. inference-time and defense timing (training-time vs. inference-time, linking each class of threat to the stage at which it can be mitigated.
