We first define the scope of VLA safety, distinguishing it from text-only LLM safety and classical robotic safety, and review the foundations of VLA models, including architectures, training paradigms, and inference mechanisms. We then examine the literature through four lenses: Attacks, Defenses, Evaluation, and Deployment. We survey training-time threats such as data poisoning and backdoors, as well as inference-time attacks including adversarial patches, cross-modal perturbations, semantic jailbreaks, and freezing attacks. We review training-time and runtime defenses, analyze existing benchmarks and metrics, and discuss safety challenges across six deployment domains. Finally, we highlight key open problems, including certified robustness for embodied trajectories, physically realizable defenses, safety-aware training, unified runtime safety architectures, and standardized evaluation.

</details>


### [122] [Cooptimizing Safety and Performance Using Safety Value-Constrained Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23863)
*Hao Wang,Nam Nguyen,Armand Jordana,Ludovic Righetti,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合安全值函数终端约束的MPC框架，通过在规划时域末端强制执行控制不变集成员约束，实现实时生成高性能且可证明安全的轨迹，并在Flexiv Rizon 10s机械臂上通过仿真和硬件实验验证了其相比标准MPC和反应式安全滤波方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MPC的有限规划时域无法保证超越时域的长期安全性，而传统终端集构造依赖于局部线性化或保守近似，导致安全性保证过于保守或表达能力有限。

Method: 在MPC中引入基于可达性分析的安全值函数作为终端约束，强制终端状态属于控制不变安全集；在精确安全值函数和可行初始条件下证明递归可行性，确保持续安全性。

Result: 在Flexiv Rizon 10s机械臂上的仿真和硬件实验表明，该方法相比标准状态约束MPC和反应式安全滤波，在约束满足度和鲁棒性上均有提升，同时保持相当的任务性能。

Conclusion: 基于安全值函数的终端约束MPC框架能够在不牺牲性能的前提下，提供更宽松、表达能力更强的安全性保证，是实现安全关键自主系统高效控制的有效方案。

Abstract: Autonomous systems are increasingly deployed in real-world environments, where they must achieve high performance while maintaining safety under state and input constraints. Although Model Predictive Control (MPC) provides a principled framework for constrained optimal control, guaranteeing safety beyond its finite planning horizon remains a fundamental challenge. In this work, we augment MPC with a safety value function-based terminal constraint that enforces membership in a control-invariant safe set at the end of each planning horizon. This formulation enables real-time synthesis of trajectories that are both high-performing and provably safe. We show that, under an exact safety value function and a feasible initialization, the proposed MPC scheme is recursively feasible, thereby ensuring persistent safety. In contrast to traditional terminal set constructions that rely on local linearizations or conservative approximations, our approach incorporates a reachability-based safety value function for terminal constraints, yielding less conservative and more expressive safety guarantees. We validate the proposed framework through simulation and hardware experiments on a Flexiv Rizon 10s manipulator. Results demonstrate improved constraint satisfaction and robustness compared to standard state-constrained MPC and reactive safety filtering, while maintaining competitive task performance. The full implementation and experiments are available on the project website.

</details>


### [123] [Multi-Robot Motions in Milliseconds: Vector-Accelerated Primitives for Sampling-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23960)
*James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将向量加速运动规划（VAMP）框架扩展到多机器人运动规划（MRMP），开发了两个向量加速原语（多机器人MotionValidation和FindFirstConflict），在纯多机器人运动验证测试中实现超过1100倍的加速，并在多种MRMP算法中实现超过850倍的规划时间加速，部分场景可在毫秒级求解多机器人规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将VAMP框架从单机器人扩展至多机器人领域，利用SIMD并行计算能力加速多机器人运动规划过程，解决多机器人运动规划计算效率低下的问题。

Method: 基于VAMP框架，开发了两个针对多机器人场景的向量加速原语：多机器人MotionValidation（MotVal）和FindFirstConflict（FFC），利用SIMD并行加速运动有效性验证和冲突检测；将多个代表性MRMP算法改造以兼容这些新原语。

Result: 纯多机器人运动验证测试实现超过1100倍的速度提升；在机械臂、刚体和异构机器人团队的多种场景中，许多情况下的规划时间加速比超过850倍，部分实例可在毫秒级完成多机器人规划。

Conclusion: VAMP框架可有效扩展至多机器人运动规划，向量加速原语能在大幅提升计算效率的同时兼容多种主流MRMP算法，为实时多机器人规划提供了可行的加速方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend the recent Vector-Accelerated Motion Planning (VAMP) framework to multi-robot motion planning (MRMP). We develop two vector-accelerated primitives, multi-robot MotionValidation (MotVal) and FindFirstConflict (FFC), which exploit SIMD parallelism within the multi-robot domain. On pure multi-robot motion validation tests, this achieves over 1100X speedup in validation time. Additionally, we modify a representative set of MRMP algorithms to use these new primitives. The relative speedup for each algorithm is studied on scenarios with manipulator, rigid body, and heterogeneous teams with some instances producing multi-robot solutions in the order of milliseconds and, in many cases, shows planning time speedups of over 850X.

</details>


### [124] [Designing Robots to Support Parent-Child Connections: Opportunities Through Robot-Mediated Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23976)
*Michael F Xu,Bengisu Cagiltay,Yaxin Hu,Anjun Zhu,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过两项研究（6个家庭的居家技术探针研究和20个家庭的实验室研究）探讨了机器人辅助沟通工具如何通过不同行为策略（被动、反应、主动）和通信模式（同步、异步）促进5-12岁儿童家庭中的亲子互动与连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术进步往往取代人际互动，而家庭连接感对个体福祉、韧性和健康家庭功能有积极影响。本研究旨在探索机器人辅助沟通工具如何创造新的家庭连接机会。

Method: 进行了两项研究：研究一通过居家技术探针与6个家庭探索设计空间；研究二在实验室中与20个家庭进行对照实验，系统考察机器人行为策略（被动、反应、主动）和通信模式（同步、异步）两个交互设计维度对亲子互动和连接的影响。

Result: 揭示了家长和儿童如何挪用机器人介导的交流方式，在主动性、时机和隐私方面经历的紧张关系，以及他们设想的支持日常连接的机会。

Conclusion: 机器人辅助沟通工具的设计需要平衡主动性与控制权、同步与异步的时序需求、以及隐私保护，才能有效促进家庭日常连接。

Abstract: The sense of family connectedness may support positive outcomes including individual well-being, resilience, and healthy family functioning. However, as technologies advance, they often replace human-human interactions instead of nurturing them. In this work, we investigate how robot-facilitated communication tools might instead create new opportunities for family connection. We conducted two studies with families with children aged 5-12. We first explored the design space through in-home technology probe sessions with six families. These probes inspired us to explore two key interaction design dimensions: the robot's behavior strategy (passive, reactive, proactive) and the mode of communication (synchronous, asynchronous). We then conducted a laboratory study with 20 families to examine how the two dimensions shaped parent-child interaction and connection. Our findings characterize how parents and children appropriated robot-mediated exchanges, the tensions they experienced around initiative, timing, and privacy, and the opportunities they envisioned for supporting everyday connectedness.

</details>


### [125] [Supporting Family-School Partnerships with Robot-Facilitated Home-Based Activities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23978)
*Michael F Xu,Qiyao Yang,Heather Kirkorian,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探索了将社交机器人引入家庭环境以支持家校合作的新方法。通过访谈和协同设计，开发了支持家庭与学校话题沟通的机器人系统，并在10个家庭中进行了为期一周的实地研究。研究展示了家庭如何将机器人融入日常生活、家长引导风格如何影响使用，以及机器人的有用性和挑战。研究贡献包括实证见解、模块化系统以及家庭-儿童-机器人交互的设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家校合作对儿童发展至关重要，但家庭常面临时间限制、沟通碎片化、缺乏有意义参与机会等障碍。为促进更广泛的家校合作，探索将社交机器人引入家庭环境以支持家庭活动的创新方法。

Method: 通过访谈和协同设计了解家长和儿童的需求，据此设计并开发了支持家庭关于学校话题沟通的机器人系统；随后在10个家庭中进行了为期一周的实地研究评估。

Result: 发现家庭将机器人融入日常生活的方式；家长引导风格对机器人使用方式的影响；用户对机器人有用性和挑战的感知。

Conclusion: 研究贡献包括实证见解、模块化系统设计以及家庭-儿童-机器人交互的设计启示；同时讨论了伦理和隐私考量，拓展了支持家校合作的技术设计空间。

Abstract: Family-school partnerships (FSP) are critical to children's development, yet families often face barriers such as time constraints, fragmented communication, and limited opportunities for meaningful engagement. As a step toward facilitating broader family-school partnerships, we explore a novel approach that integrates a social robot into family settings, specifically supporting home-based activities. Through interviews and co-design sessions, we designed and developed a robotic system informed by both parents and children, that supported, among other interactions, family communication about school topics. We evaluated the robot in a week-long, in-home study with 10 families. Our findings show how families integrated the robot into daily life, how parental facilitation styles shaped use, and how families perceived both the helpfulness and challenges of the robot. We contribute empirical insights, a modular system, and design implications for family- and child-robot interactions. We discuss ethical and privacy considerations, and broaden the design space for technologies supporting family-school partnerships.

</details>


### [126] [Betting for Sim-to-Real Performance Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24018)
*Zaid Mahboob,Yujia Chen,Bowen Weng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于赌注机制（betting mechanism）的机器人性能评估方法，能够在物理实验极度受限的条件下，从仿真数据中高效、准确地推断真实世界行为，并提供了理论保证与实用决策规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理机器人实验成本高昂、耗时且受安全限制，导致真实世界试验数据稀缺。现有方法（如方差缩减、偏差校正）虽有一定效果，但仍需更高效、更准确的评估框架来支持算法基准测试、设计比较、控制器验证及认证决策。

Method: 从赌注视角审视性能评估问题：建立赌注机制产生准确高效估计的理论条件，证明其可超越蒙特卡洛估计器；给出理想赌注的构建方式，并提出具有理论依据且可实践近似的赌博策略，同时提供诊断这些近似策略是否有效的具体决策规则。

Result: 通过合成示例和跨保真度计算模拟器验证了方法的有效性。在机器人抓取精度推断案例中，利用一组合成分布成功推断真实世界机械臂的拾放精度，展示了该方法在仿真到现实迁移中的可行性。

Conclusion: 赌注机制为仿真到现实的性能评估提供了一种新颖且互补的视角，具有理论保证和实践可行性，代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of robot performance evaluation, focusing on how to obtain accurate and efficient estimates of real-world behavior under severe constraints on physical experimentation. Such estimates are essential for benchmarking algorithms, comparing design alternatives, validating controllers, and supporting certification or regulatory decision-making, yet real-world testing with physical robots is often expensive, time-consuming, and safety-limited. To mitigate the scarcity of real-world trials, sim-to-real methodologies are commonly employed, using low-cost simulators to inform, supplement, or prioritize physical experiments. Departing from (and complementary to) existing approaches in variance reduction (e.g., importance-sampling variants) or bias-correction (e.g., through prediction-powered inference or learned control variates), we examine this performance-evaluation problem through the lens of betting. We establish theoretical conditions under which a betting mechanism can yield accurate and efficient estimates (provably outperforming the Monte Carlo estimator) and we characterize how such bets should be constructed. We further develop theoretically grounded yet practically implementable approximations of the ideal bet, and we provide concrete decision rules that diagnose when these approximate betting strategies are working as intended. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods using both synthetic examples and cross-fidelity computational simulators. Notably, we also showcase an illustrative case in which a group of synthetic distributions are used to infer the real-world pick-and-place accuracy of a robotic manipulator, a seemingly unconventional sim-to-real transfer that becomes natural and feasible under the proposed betting perspective. Programs for reproducing empirical results are available at https://github.com/ISUSAIL/Bet4Sim2Real.

</details>


### [127] [Event-based SLAM Benchmark for High-Speed Maneuvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24033)
*Sheng Zhong,Junkai Niu,Guillermo Gallego,Kaizhen Sun,Yang Yi,Zhiqiang Miao,Dewen Hu,Yaonan Wang,Davide Scaramuzza,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对现有事件相机视觉里程计/视觉惯性里程计方法进行全面分析，指出其局限性，并提出了一个名为EvSLAM的基准测试框架，该框架包含多样化的数据采集平台、极端光照场景和高速机动模式，以及新的评估指标，用于公平评估事件相机在任意激进机动下的状态估计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机方法虽然能缓解高速运动模糊，但存在诸多局限：部分方法仅适用于前向平行快速抖动场景并需要局部地图始终在视野内，而其他方法假设纯三自由度旋转，无法推广到线速度和角速度都可能很大的六自由度运动。因此，事件相机在任意激进机动下的状态估计问题尚未完全解决。

Method: 对当前最先进的事件相机视觉里程计/视觉惯性里程计方法进行深入分析，指出现有公开数据集的不足；提出EvSLAM基准测试框架，特点是数据采集平台多样化、包含极端光照场景和广泛的挑战性运动模式，并给出高速机动的明确定义，同时引入新的评估指标以公平评估事件相机解决方案的极限。

Result: 通过EvSLAM框架对最先进方法进行基准测试，得出了关于最优架构和持续存在的挑战的见解。

Conclusion: 事件相机在高速机动场景下的状态估计仍然是一个未完全解决的问题，EvSLAM框架为公平评估和比较不同方法提供了重要工具，并揭示了现有方法的不足和未来研究方向。

Abstract: Event-based cameras are bio-inspired sensors with pixels that independently and asynchronously respond to brightness changes at microsecond resolution, offering the potential to handle visual tasks in high-speed maneuvering scenarios. Existing event-based approaches, although successful in mitigating motion blur caused by high-speed maneuvers, suffer from many limitations. Some of them highlight a success of pose tracking for a fronto-parallel fast shaking camera closed to the structure, while others assume pure (optionally aggressive) three-degree-of-freedom rotations. The former requires persistent local map visibility within the field of view (FOV), whereas the latter fails to generalize to six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) motions where both linear and angular velocities may be large. Consequently, current successes do not fully demonstrate that event-based state estimation under arbitrary aggressive maneuvers is a fully solved problem. To quantitatively assess the extent to which the potential of event cameras has been unlocked, we conduct a thorough analysis of state-of-the-art (SOTA) event-based visual odometry (VO)/visual-inertial odometry (VIO) methods and report shortcomings in current public datasets. Furthermore, we introduce a benchmarking framework for event-based state estimation, called EvSLAM, characterized by sufficient variation in data collection platforms, diverse extreme lighting scenarios, and a wide scope of challenging motion patterns under a clear and rigorous definition of high-speed maneuvers for mobile robots, along with a novel evaluation metric designed to fairly assess the operational limits of event-based solutions. This framework benchmarks state-of-the-art methods, yielding insights into optimal architectures and persistent challenges.

</details>


### [128] [Trajectory Planning for an Articulated Commercial Vehicle using Model Predictive Contouring Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24064)
*A. J. Aertssen,R. G. M. Huisman,I. J. M. Besselink,J. Elfring,M. J. G. van de Molengraft*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型预测轮廓控制（MPCC）的 articulated 商用车辆（牵引车-半挂车）轨迹规划方法。针对 MPCC 在大型车辆中存在的路径差异、倒车不稳定及易发生折叠等问题，扩展了 MPCC 以支持场景依赖的锚点加权，并加入了前后轴及挂车轴的显式道路边界约束。仿真和实车测试验证了该方法在正向和反向行驶中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 MPCC 方法主要针对乘用车设计，不适用于牵引车-半挂车这类大型铰接车辆：车辆尺寸大、挂车路径与牵引车不同、倒车时不稳定且易发生折叠；实际驾驶场景中需要根据不同工况（如对接装载时优先挂车位置、充电停车时优先牵引车位置）对不同锚点进行差异化加权。

Method: 扩展 MPCC 框架，引入场景依赖的锚点（牵引车/挂车）加权机制，并添加牵引车前轴、后轴及挂车轴的显式道路边界约束，确保所有车轮保持在可行驶区域内。

Result: 仿真结果显示该方法能成功完成典型物流场景的正向和反向行驶任务；分析了优化参数对轨迹的影响；全尺寸原型实车测试验证了方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的扩展 MPCC 方法能有效解决铰接式商用车辆在复杂场景下的轨迹规划问题，兼顾不同工况下的锚点优先级和道路边界约束，具有良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: This paper presents a trajectory planning method for articulated commercial vehicles, specifically tractor-semitrailers, based on Model Predictive Contouring Control (MPCC). Although MPCC has proven effective for passenger cars, it is generally ill-suited for tractor-semitrailers. These vehicles are significantly larger, the semitrailer follows a different path than the tractor, and reversing maneuvers are unstable and prone to jackknifing. Furthermore, practical driving scenarios often require scenario-dependent prioritization of different vehicle `anchor points', e.g., prioritizing the semitrailer position during docking or the tractor position when parking to charge. Therefore, we extend MPCC to enable scenario-dependent weighting of these anchor points and incorporate explicit road-boundary constraints for the front and rear tractor axles and the semitrailer axle, thereby ensuring that all considered wheels remain within the drivable area. The simulation results demonstrate the successful navigation of a representative logistic scenario in both forward and reverse direction. Furthermore, the influence of the optimization parameters on the trajectories is analyzed, providing insights into controlling the vehicle behavior. Finally, first tests using a full-scale prototype vehicle show the practical applicability of the approach.

</details>


### [129] [OpenPodcar2: a robust, ROS2 vehicle for self-driving research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24242)
*Rakshit Soni,Chris Waltham,Md Umar Ibrahim,Mark Crampton,Charles Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenPodcar2 是一个基于商用 mobility scooter 改造的开源自动驾驶车辆平台，具备 ROS2 接口、仿真支持、完整导航堆栈，总成本约 2000-7000 美元，可用于研究或实际部署（如最后一英里自动驾驶出租车或货物运输）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶研究平台成本高昂、难以复现，缺乏既适用于研究又能用于实际部署的低成本、开源且安全的解决方案。作者旨在改进之前的 OpenPodcar 设计，提供更鲁棒的电子系统和 ROS2 接口，以弥合研究与部署之间的鸿沟。

Method: 基于商用硬顶 mobility scooter 改装，集成通用 OSH R4 机电控制板；提供 Gazebo 仿真；实现 ROS2 nav2 导航堆栈（SLAM + 运动规划与控制）；完整开源 BOM 和装配说明。

Result: 成品平台可载人（或等效载荷）以最高 15km/h 速度行驶；新零件总成本约 7000 美元，使用二手捐赠车辆则约 2000 美元；体积小巧安全，可停放于标准研究实验室。

Conclusion: OpenPodcar2 在现实世界实用性、安全性、成本和鲁棒性之间取得了良好平衡，可作为自动驾驶研究的可复现、低门槛平台，并具备一定的实际部署潜力。

Abstract: OpenPodcar2 is a robust, ROS2-interfaced, low-cost, open source hardware and software, autonomous vehicle platform based on an off-the-shelf, hard-canopy, mobility scooter donor vehicle. It is a modification of the previous OpenPodcar design, which extends it with robust electronics and ROS2 interfacing, to enable both research and also potential deployment use cases. The platform consists of (a) hardware components: documented as a bill of materials and build instructions; (b) integration to the general purpose OSH R4 mechatronics board and a Gazebo simulation of the vehicle, both presenting a common ROS2 interface (c) higher-level ROS2 software implementations and configurations of standard robot autonomous planning and control, including the nav2 stack which performs SLAM and enacts commands to drive the vehicle from a current to a desired pose around obstacles. OpenPodcar2 can transport a human passenger or similar load at speeds up to 15km/h, for example for use as a last-mile autonomous taxi service or to transport delivery containers similarly around a city center. It is small and safe enough to be parked in a standard research lab robust enough for some deployment cases. Total build cost was around 7,000USD from new components, or 2,000USD with a used Donor Vehicle. OpenPodcar2 thus provides a research balance between real world utility, safety, cost and robustness.

</details>


### [130] [Projected Attainable Speed Space: A Driving Efficiency Metric Connecting Instantaneous Evaluation to Travel Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24295)
*Xiaohua Zhao,Zhaowei Huang,Chen Chen,Haiyi Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Projected Attainable Speed Space (PASS)模型，一种统一框架用于评估自动驾驶中瞬时和行程级别的驾驶效率，通过集成运动学和空间交通信息，实现跨时间尺度的一致性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有瞬时效率指标（如速度、相对速度、车距）难以捕获交通上下文，且无法保证瞬时输出与行程级结果之间的一致性，阻碍了自动驾驶的高效决策。

Method: PASS模型通过两个耦合元素表征瞬时效率：可用加速空间（基于理想化追赶机动计算的投影可达速度）和该空间的利用程度（可用加速空间的时间变化率）。时间聚合的PASS作为行程级指标，通过驾驶模拟实验数据标定参数，最大化与观测旅行时间的一致性。

Result: 在10个变道事件中，时间聚合PASS与旅行时间之间的平均决定系数为0.913，验证了模型在瞬时与行程级时间尺度上的一致性评估能力。

Conclusion: PASS提供了一个统一、物理基础的框架，支持自动驾驶的实时决策和长期性能分析，有效弥合了瞬时效率与行程效率之间的尺度鸿沟。

Abstract: Inefficient driving behaviors, such as overly conservative yielding, remain a key obstacle to deployment of autonomous vehicles (AVs). Instantaneous driving efficiency metrics are crucial for self-driving decision-making because they affect real-time performance evaluation and control optimization. However, commonly used indicators, including speed, relative speed, and inter-vehicle distance, are limited in capturing traffic context and in ensuring consistency between instantaneous outputs and travel-level outcomes. This study proposes the Projected Attainable Speed Space (PASS) model, a unified framework for driving efficiency assessment across instantaneous and travel-level analyses by integrating kinematic and spatial traffic information. PASS characterizes instantaneous driving efficiency with two coupled elements: potential for speed improvement (available acceleration space) and response to that potential (utilization of available acceleration space). Available acceleration space is referenced to projected attainable speed, derived from an idealized catch-up maneuver using relative speed and spacing to the leading vehicle; utilization is represented by the temporal change in available acceleration space. To ensure cross-scale consistency, time-aggregated PASS is defined as a travel-level efficiency metric. Trajectory data from a driving simulation experiment are used for parameter calibration to maximize agreement between time-aggregated PASS and observed travel times. Across 10 lane-change events, results show strong consistency, with an average coefficient of determination of 0.913, validating PASS for consistent efficiency evaluation across instantaneous and travel-level temporal scales. This study provides a unified, physically grounded framework that supports real-time decision-making and long-term performance analysis in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [131] [Pedestrians play chicken with an autonomous vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24384)
*Rakshit Soni,Charles Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次通过真实自动驾驶车辆与人类行人实验，验证了使用博弈论中的序贯博弈模型（Sequential Chicken）解决自动驾驶车辆无条件让行导致的“冻结机器人问题”，实验表明行人行为符合该模型，且行人更关注避免接近空间（proxemic personal space）侵犯而非实际碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆通常被编程为无条件让行行人以保障安全，但这种设计会导致“冻结机器人问题”——行人学会在所有交互中主张优先权，导致车辆停滞不前。现有序贯博弈模型理论上表明可以通过发出“极小碰撞风险”或“侵入个人空间”的威胁来解决该问题，但缺乏真实车辆与行人的实验验证。

Method: 使用真实自动驾驶车辆与人类行人进行交互实验，在受控安全条件下观察行人行为，并以序贯博弈模型（Sequential Chicken）对实验数据进行拟合分析。

Result: 行人行为能够很好地拟合序贯博弈模型，且行人的碰撞时间价值（time value of collision）较低，表明他们更倾向于通过规划避免个人空间侵犯（proxemic personal space），而非单纯避免实际碰撞。

Conclusion: 序贯博弈模型可有效用于自动驾驶车辆与行人交互策略设计，通过可信的微小风险威胁而非无条件让行，能在保证安全的同时避免车辆停滞，提升交通效率。

Abstract: Automated vehicles (AVs) are commonly programmed to yield unconditionally to pedestrians in the interest of safety. However, this design choice can give rise to the Freezing Robot Problem in which pedestrians learn to assert priority at every interaction, causing vehicles to stall and make no progress. The game theoretic Sequential Chicken model has shown that, like human drivers, AVs can resolve this problem by trading credible threats of very small risks of collision or larger risks of less severe invasion of personal space against the value of time due to yielding delays. This paper presents the first demonstration and evaluation of this approach using a real AV with human subjects and shows that pedestrian behavior under experimentally constrained safety conditions can be well fitted by Sequential Chicken, with a low time value of collision, suggestive of their planning to avoid proxemic personal space penalties as well as actual collisions.

</details>


### [132] [FreqCache: Accelerating Embodied VLN Models with Adaptive Frequency-Guided Token Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24391)
*Zihao Zheng,Xingyue Zhou,Zhihao Mao,Songyu Sun,Lingyue Zhang,Yulong Ao,Yupu Feng,Qiongqiong Zhang,Yonghua Lin,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLN模型中视觉领域token缓存方法在视角迁移、边缘信息缺失和缓存预算适应性方面的问题，本文提出基于频域引导的FreqCache框架，利用频域不变性和可分析性实现最优token缓存的建立、刷新和自适应调整，取得1.59倍加速且开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN模型导航精度高但计算开销大，token缓存虽是一种有前景的无训练加速策略，但直接套用视觉领域的缓存方法存在三大挑战：视角迁移时失效、忽视边缘信息、缺乏时序自适应调整能力。

Method: 通过频域分析发现上述挑战在频域中具有不变性和可分析性，据此提出FreqCache框架，利用频域固有属性实现token缓存的建立、刷新和自适应调整。

Result: FreqCache实现1.59倍加速，且额外开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 频域方法能有效解决VLN中token缓存的适配问题，为VLN模型的高效推理提供新思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Navigation (VLN) models exhibit excellent navigation accuracy but incur high computational overhead. Token caching has emerged as a promising training-free strategy to reduce this cost by reusing token computation results; however, existing token caching approaches rely on visual domain methods for cacheable token selection, leading to challenges when adapted to VLN models. 1) Visual domain methods become invalid when there is viewpoint migration. 2) Visual domain methods neglect critical edge information without the aid of additional algorithms. 3) Visual domain methods overlook the temporal variation of scenarios and lack adjustability in cache budgets. In this paper, we develop detailed analyses and find that the impacts of these challenges exhibit invariance and analyzability in the frequency domain. Based on these, we propose a frequency-guided token caching framework, called FreqCache. Utilizing the inherent properties of the frequency domain, FreqCache achieves optimal token cache establishment, refreshment, and adaptive adjustment. Experiments show that FreqCache achieves 1.59x speedup with ignorable overhead, showing the effect of integrating frequency domain methods in VLN token caching.

</details>


### [133] [Characterizing Vision-Language-Action Models across XPUs: Constraints and Acceleration for On-Robot Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24447)
*Kaijun Zhou,Qiwei Chen,Da Peng,Zhiyang Li,Xijun Li,Jinyu Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了低成本VLA模型在异构边缘加速器上的部署，揭示了计算/内存两阶段推理模式，并提出了DP-Cache和V-AEFusion优化方法，在GPU上实现2.9倍加速，边缘NPU上实现6倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型部署研究多依赖桌面级GPU，忽视了异构边缘加速器（GPU/XPU/NPU）带来的权衡与机会，且实际机器人部署受实时性、成本和能耗的严格约束。

Method: 1) 构建跨加速器排行榜，在CET（成本、能耗、时间）指标下评估模型-硬件配对；2) 通过深入性能分析揭示两阶段推理模式（计算密集的VLM骨干+内存密集的Action Expert）；3) 提出DP-Cache减少扩散冗余和V-AEFusion实现异步流水线并行。

Result: DP-Cache和V-AEFusion在GPU上实现最高2.9倍加速，在边缘NPU上实现最高6倍加速，且成功率仅轻微下降。

Conclusion: 选择合适的边缘设备可在满足控制率约束的同时比旗舰GPU更具成本/能效优势；通过理解推理模式并针对性优化，可显著提升VLA模型在异构硬件上的部署效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are promising for generalist robot control, but on-robot deployment is bottlenecked by real-time inference under tight cost and energy budgets. Most prior evaluations rely on desktop-grade GPUs, obscuring the trade-offs and opportunities offered by heterogeneous edge accelerators (GPUs/XPUs/NPUs). We present a systematic analysis for low-cost VLA deployment via model-hardware co-characterization. First, we build a cross-accelerator leaderboard and evaluate model-hardware pairs under CET (Cost, Energy, Time), showing that right-sized edge devices can be more cost-/energy-efficient than flagship GPUs while meeting control-rate constraints. Second, using in-depth profiling, we uncover a consistent two-phase inference pattern: a compute-bound VLM backbone followed by a memory-bound Action Expert, which induces phase-dependent underutilization and hardware inefficiency. Finally, guided by these insights, we propose DP-Cache and V-AEFusion to reduce diffusion redundancy and enable asynchronous pipeline parallelism, achieving up to 2.9x speedup on GPUs and 6x on edge NPUs with only marginal success degradation. The example leaderboard website is available at: https://vla-leaderboard-01.vercel.app/.

</details>


### [134] [SPLIT: Separating Physical-Contact via Latent Arithmetic in Image-Based Tactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24449)
*Wadhah Zai El Amri,Nicolás Navarro-Guerrero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPLIT提出了一种基于隐空间算术策略的图像式触觉传感器仿真方法，通过解耦接触几何与传感器光学特性，实现了跨传感器适配、快速推理、双向仿真等功能，加速了机器人触觉感知研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练机器人触觉感知模型需要大量真实交互数据，但由于物理复杂性和变异性，获取这些数据仍然困难，因此需要高效的触觉传感器仿真方法来加速研究进程。

Method: 提出SPLIT方法，核心是隐空间算术策略，显式解耦接触几何与传感器光学特性；结合标定的有限元法柔体网格仿真（可变分辨率），支持双向仿真（形变网格→图像，图像→网格重建）。

Result: SPLIT能够适应不同DIGIT传感器背景，无需完整模型重训练即可将数据迁移到GelSight R1.5等不同传感器；推理速度优于现有替代方法。

Conclusion: SPLIT作为一种多功能触觉传感器仿真工具，可加速机器人触觉感知领域的研究进展。

Abstract: Training machine learning models for robotic tactile sensing requires vast amounts of data, yet obtaining realistic interaction data remains a challenge due to physical complexity and variability. Simulating tactile sensors is thus a crucial step in accelerating progress. This paper presents SPLIT, a novel method for simulating image-based tactile sensors, with a primary focus on the DIGIT sensor. Central to our approach is a latent space arithmetic strategy that explicitly disentangles contact geometry from sensor-specific optical properties. Unlike methods that require recalibration for every new unit, this disentanglement allows SPLIT to adapt to diverse DIGIT backgrounds and even transfer data to distinct sensors like the GelSight R1.5 without full model retraining. Beyond this adaptability, our approach achieves faster inference speeds than existing alternatives. Furthermore, we provide a calibrated finite element method (FEM) soft-body mesh simulation with variable resolution, offering a tunable trade-off between speed and fidelity. Additionally, our algorithm supports bidirectional simulation, allowing for both the generation of realistic images from deformation meshes and the reconstruction of meshes from tactile images. This versatility makes SPLIT a valuable tool for accelerating progress in robotic tactile sensing research.

</details>


### [135] [Guiding Vector Field Generation via Score-based Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24487)
*Zirui Chen,Shiliang Guo,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分数生成模型的引导向量场框架（SGVF），通过从数据分布中直接学习向量场，解决了传统GVF无法处理无序、多分支路径的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统引导向量场方法假设路径是平滑有序的曲线，无法处理无序、多分支或由概率模型生成的路径，需要一种能从数据分布中直接学习向量场的统一框架。

Method: 利用基于分数的生成模型，从点云数据中通过单位范数、正交性和方向一致性损失学习切向量场，建立扩散模型中分数消失与GVF奇异点之间的对应关系。

Result: 在平面环境机器人导航实验中，SGVF在经典GVF失败的多分支、复杂拓扑场景下实现了可靠的路径跟随。

Conclusion: SGVF消除了对临时路径分割的依赖，能够处理分支和伪流形等复杂拓扑，展示了生成建模与几何控制之间桥接的潜力。

Abstract: Guiding Vector Fields (GVFs) are a powerful tool for robotic path following. However, classical methods assume smooth, ordered curves and fail when paths are unordered, multi-branch, or generated by probabilistic models. We propose a unified framework, termed the Score-Induced Guiding Vector Field (SGVF), which leverages score-based generative modeling to construct vector fields directly from data distributions. SGVF learns tangent fields from point clouds with unit-norm, orthogonality, and directional-consistency losses, ensuring geometric fidelity and control feasibility. This approach removes the reliance on ad-hoc path segmentation and enables guidance along complex topologies such as branching and pseudo-manifolds. The study establishes a correspondence between score vanishing in diffusion models and GVF singularities and highlights representational capacity near sharp path curvatures. Experiments on robotic navigation in planar environments demonstrate that SGVF achieves reliable path following in scenarios where classical GVFs fail, underscoring its potential as a bridge between generative modeling and geometric control. Code and experiment video are available at https://github.com/czr-gif/Guiding-Vector-Field-Generation-via-Score-based-Diffusion-Model.

</details>


### [136] [Hybrid A*-Based Reverse Path-Planning of a Vehicle with Trailer System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24606)
*Xincheng Cao,Haochong Chen,Bilin Aksun-Guvenc,Levent Guvenc,Brian Link,Peter J Richmond,Dokyung Yim,Shihong Fan,John Harber*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的Hybrid A*算法，用于解决带拖车车辆的倒车入库问题，该算法能够适应不同系统配置下的转向限制以防止折刀现象，并具备碰撞规避功能。仿真案例验证了所提方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带拖车车辆的倒车入库对驾驶员极具挑战性，现有路径规划方法缺乏碰撞规避功能，因此需要一种既能处理车辆-拖车系统运动学，又能同时考虑碰撞避免的算法。

Method: 提出一种改进的Hybrid A*算法，核心创新包括：(1) 根据系统配置动态调整转向输入限制以防止折刀现象；(2) 在标准Hybrid A*基础上增加碰撞规避功能。

Result: 通过仿真案例研究，证明了所提出的方法在带拖车车辆倒车入库场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该改进算法成功解决了带拖车系统倒车路径规划中的折刀预防和碰撞规避问题，弥补了现有方法的不足。

Abstract: Reverse parking maneuvering of a vehicle with trailer system is a difficult task to complete for human drivers due to the multi-body nature of the system and the unintuitive controls required to orientate the trailer properly. The problem is complicated with the presence of other vehicles that the trailer and its connected vehicle must avoid during the reverse parking maneuver. While path planning methods in reverse motion for vehicles with trailers exist, there is a lack of results that also offer collision avoidance as part of the algorithm. This paper hence proposes a modified Hybrid A*-based algorithm that can accommodate the vehicle-trailer system as well as collision avoidance considerations with the other vehicles and obstacles in the parking environment. One of the novelties of this proposed approach is its adaptability to the vehicle with trailer system, where limits of usable steering input that prevent the occurrence of jackknife incidents vary with respect to system configuration. The other contribution is the addition of the collision avoidance functionality which the standard Hybrid A* algorithm lacks. The method is developed and presented first, followed by simulation case studies to demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [137] [Real-time windrow detection from onboard tractor sensors for automated following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24628)
*Lorenz Gunreben,Nico Heider,Sebastian Zürner,Martin Schieck,Bogdan Franczyk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合立体视觉与LiDAR的多模态数据集，用于商用青贮收割中的草垄检测。基于该数据集，在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上实现了实时（>20 Hz）的质心草垄跟踪方法。在4-10米关键引导范围内，立体视觉与LiDAR深度测量一致性达0.965±0.021，表明低成本立体传感器可接近LiDAR性能。开源ROS 2管道为无GPS草垄检测提供了可重复基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用草垄检测系统为专有设计，限制了透明度和开放自主青贮收割研究的发展，因此需要开源的多模态数据集和可复现的检测方法。

Method: 采集了拖拉机传感器在真实打捆作业中的立体视觉与LiDAR多模态数据，含GNSS轨迹，以ROS 2 Humble格式发布。在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上实现基于质心的实时草垄跟踪方法（>20 Hz）。

Result: 在4-10米关键引导范围内，立体视觉与LiDAR深度测量一致性达0.965±0.021，表明低成本立体传感器性能接近LiDAR；系统实现了>20 Hz的实时处理速度。

Conclusion: 开源ROS 2管道为无GPS草垄检测提供了可重复基准，支持实用自主青贮收割系统的开发。

Abstract: Proprietary design in commercial windrow-detection systems restricts transparency and limits progress in open autonomous forage-harvesting research. We present a multi-modal dataset combining stereo vision and LiDAR from tractor-mounted sensors during real baling operations. The dataset includes synchronized sensor data with GNSS trajectories, partly released as ROS2 Humble bags on Zenodo, with additional data available on request. Using this dataset, we implement a real-time (>20 Hz) centroid-based windrow-following method on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. Across the critical 4-10 m guidance range, stereo and LiDAR depth measurements show strong agreement (0.965 +/- 0.021), indicating that low-cost stereo sensors can approach LiDAR performance. Our open-source ROS 2 pipeline provides a reproducible benchmark for GPS-free windrow detection and supports development of practical autonomous forage-harvesting systems. Dataset: https://zenodo.org/records/17486318

</details>


### [138] [Computational Design and Co-Robotic Fabrication for Material Reuse in Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24648)
*Arash Adel,Daniel Ruan,Ruxin Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将数据驱动计算设计与自适应人机协作制造装配相结合的综合框架，通过实际案例验证了该框架能够利用回收木材实现非常规结构建造，为促进建筑材料循环再利用提供了新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化和资源枯竭要求建筑行业从线性的"取-制-用-弃"范式转向循环、低废料实践。材料再利用可减少原材料开采、减少废物并延长如木材等碳封存材料的使用寿命，但实现这一潜力需要解决设计和施工中适应异构、回收材料库存的技术和物流挑战。

Method: 提出了一个集成框架，将数据驱动计算设计与反馈驱动的自适应人机协作制造和装配相结合，以实现在必要时补充现成标准木材的情况下，利用不同长度和几何形状的回收木材建造非常规结构。通过Timbrelyn案例研究安装项目进行验证。

Result: 通过Timbrelyn实际案例研究，验证了该框架能够有效处理库存约束和回收材料的不确定性，实现了从设计到制造的工作流程整合，展示了木材再利用如何能够启发和增强建筑表现力。

Conclusion: 本研究促进了集成设计到制造工作流程的发展，推进了自适应、反馈驱动的方法来处理库存约束和回收材料不确定性，为在新建筑和结构的设计与施工中促进材料再利用做出了贡献。

Abstract: Climate change and resource depletion demand a shift from the dominant linear "take-make-use-dispose" paradigm of construction toward circular, low-waste practices. Material reuse offers a promising pathway by reducing raw material extraction, mitigating waste, and extending the service lifespan of carbon-sequestering materials such as timber. Realizing this potential, however, requires addressing technical and logistical challenges across both design and construction for accommodating heterogeneous, reclaimed material inventories.
  This paper presents an integrated framework that couples data-driven computational design with feedback-driven adaptive human-robot collaborative (co-robotic) fabrication and assembly to enable the realization of nonstandard structures made from reclaimed timber of varying length and geometries, supplemented with new off-the-shelf timber when necessary. The framework is validated through Timbrelyn, a built case-study installation that demonstrates how timber reuse can inform and enhance architectural expression. This work contributes to the development of integrated design-to-fabrication workflows that advance adaptive, feedback-driven methods to handle inventory constraints and reclaimed material uncertainties, facilitating material reuse in the design and construction of new buildings and structures.

</details>


### [139] [Agent-Centric Visual Reinforcement Learning under Dynamic Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24661)
*Zhengru Fang,Yu Guo,Fei Liu,Yuang Zhang,Yihang Tao,Senkang Hu,Wenbo Ding,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉强化学习在动态视觉扰动下的脆弱性问题，提出了VDCS基准测试和ACO-MoE框架，通过信息论分析揭示了重构目标导致表征纠缠的根源，并利用专家混合架构实现感知与扰动的解耦，在多种扰动下恢复95.3%的清洁性能并达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉强化学习方法在面对动态视觉扰动（如非平稳的马尔可夫切换型退化）时性能严重下降，缺乏系统研究该问题的基准平台和有效解决方案。

Method: 1) 构建VDCS基准，在DeepMind Control Suite中引入马尔可夫切换退化模拟非平稳扰动；2) 通过信息论分析证明重构目标必然导致扰动伪影与潜在表征纠缠；3) 提出ACO-MoE框架，利用智能体为中心的恢复专家实现扰动修复和任务相关前景提取，从而在RL处理前解耦感知与扰动。

Result: 在VDCS上ACO-MoE恢复95.3%的清洁性能，在DMControl泛化基准的随机颜色和视频背景扰动上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过解耦感知与扰动的专家混合架构可有效提升视觉RL在动态扰动下的鲁棒性，信息论分析为理解重构目标局限性提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Visual reinforcement learning aims to empower an agent to learn policies from visual observations, yet it remains vulnerable to dynamic visual perturbations, such as unpredictable shifts in corruption types. To systematically study this, we introduce the Visual Degraded Control Suite (VDCS), a benchmark extending DeepMind Control Suite with Markov-switching degradations to simulate non-stationary real-world perturbations. Experiments on VDCS reveal severe performance degradation in existing methods. We theoretically prove via information-theoretic analysis that this failure stems from reconstruction-based objectives inevitably entangling perturbation artifacts into latent representations. To mitigate this negative impact, we propose Agent-Centric Observations with Mixture-of-Experts (ACO-MoE) to robustify visual RL against perturbations. The proposed framework leverages unique agent-centric restoration experts, achieving restoration from corruptions and task-relevant foreground extraction, thereby decoupling perception from perturbation before being processed by the RL agent. Extensive experiments on VDCS show our ACO-MoE outperforms strong baselines, recovering 95.3% of clean performance under challenging Markov-switching corruptions. Moreover, it achieves SOTA results on DMControl Generalization with random-color and video-background perturbations, demonstrating a high level of robustness.

</details>


### [140] [Pushing Radar Odometry Beyond the Pavement: Current Capabilities and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24674)
*Shaunak Kolhe,Peng Jiang,Maggie Wigness,Philip Osteen,Timothy Overbye,Chrisitan Ellis,Srikanth Saripalli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了雷达在非结构化越野环境中的里程计估计问题，提出了两种基线方法（Radar-KISSICP和Radar-IMU），在Great Outdoors数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达在非结构化环境（越野场景）中的里程计性能尚未得到充分研究，现有工作主要集中于城市平面环境。本文旨在探索雷达在越野场景下的潜力，并识别其面临的挑战。

Method: 提出了两种简易基线方法：1) Radar-KISSICP，通过运动补偿生成3D感知雷达点云；2) Radar-IMU，利用IMU预积分稳定扫描匹配过程。

Result: 在Great Outdoors数据集上，两种基线方法在具有挑战性的越野路线上改善了轨迹估计效果。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基线方法为越野机器人雷达里程计的未来发展提供了参考基准。

Abstract: Radar offers unique advantages for localization in unstructured environments, including robustness to weather, lighting, and airborne particulates. While most prior work has studied radar odometry in urban, largely planar settings, its performance in off-road environments remains less understood. In this paper, we investigate the potential of radar for off-road odometry estimation and identify key challenges that arise from full $SE(3)$ vehicle motion, terrain-induced ground returns, and sparse or unstable features. To address these issues, we introduce two simple baselines: Radar-KISSICP, which applies motion compensation to generate 3D-aware radar pointclouds, and Radar-IMU, which leverages IMU preintegration to stabilize scan matching. Experiments on the Great Outdoors (GO) dataset demonstrate that these baselines improve trajectory estimation in challenging routes and provide a reference point for future development of radar odometry in off-road robotics.

</details>


### [141] [Learning Human-Intention Priors from Large-Scale Human Demonstrations for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24681)
*Yifan Xie,YuAn Wang,Guangyu Chen,Jinkun Liu,Yu Sun,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoT-HRA是一个分层视觉-语言-动作框架，通过从大规模人类演示中学习人类意图先验，利用HA-2.2M数据集（从异构人类视频重建的220万条动作-语言数据），拆解操作为三个专家模块（视觉-语言、意图、精细动作），在共享注意力主干和只读键值传递机制下，实现从人类运动先验到机器人动作的有效迁移，在仿真和真实机器人任务中提升了运动合理性和鲁棒控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频蕴含丰富的操作先验知识，但直接将其用于机器人学习非常困难，因为原始观测数据纠缠了场景理解、人体运动和具体化动作，难以直接解耦和迁移。

Method: 首先构建HA-2.2M数据集，通过手部中心过滤、空间重建、时间分割和语言对齐从异构人类视频中重建出220万条动作-语言数据。然后提出MoT-HRA框架，将操作分解为三个耦合专家：视觉-语言专家预测与具体化无关的3D轨迹，意图专家以MANO风格手部运动作为隐式人体运动先验，精细动作专家将意图感知表示映射为机器人动作块。采用共享注意力主干和只读键值传递机制，使下游控制能利用人类先验同时限制对上游表示的干扰。

Result: 在手部运动生成、仿真操作和真实机器人任务上的实验表明，MoT-HRA在分布偏移下提升了运动合理性和鲁棒控制能力。

Conclusion: 通过分层分解和只读信息传递，MoT-HRA成功将人类视频中的操作先验迁移到机器人控制中，为从人类演示中学习鲁棒机器人策略提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Human videos contain rich manipulation priors, but using them for robot learning remains difficult because raw observations entangle scene understanding, human motion, and embodiment-specific action. We introduce MoT-HRA, a hierarchical vision-language-action framework that learns human-intention priors from large-scale human demonstrations. We first curate HA-2.2M, a 2.2M-episode action-language dataset reconstructed from heterogeneous human videos through hand-centric filtering, spatial reconstruction, temporal segmentation, and language alignment. On top of this dataset, MoT-HRA factorizes manipulation into three coupled experts: a vision-language expert predicts an embodiment-agnostic 3D trajectory, an intention expert models MANO-style hand motion as a latent human-motion prior, and a fine expert maps the intention-aware representation to robot action chunks. A shared-attention trunk and read-only key-value transfer allow downstream control to use human priors while limiting interference with upstream representations. Experiments on hand motion generation, simulated manipulation, and real-world robot tasks show that MoT-HRA improves motion plausibility and robust control under distribution shift.

</details>


### [142] [Passage-Aware Structural Mapping for RGB-D Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24707)
*Ali Tourani,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Saad Ejaz,David Pérez Saura,Asier Bikandi-Noya,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez,Holger Voos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向RGB-D VSLAM的通道感知结构建图方法，通过融合几何、语义和拓扑线索来检测门和可通行开口，并将其集成到vS-Graphs系统中以丰富场景图的房间连通性建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 门和通道是室内机器人导航的关键结构元素，但在现代VSLAM框架中尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 将门建模为嵌入墙体的平面实体，根据与支撑墙的共面性判断可通行性；通过两种互补策略推断通道：连续关键帧间相机-墙体交互的遍历证据积累，以及基于映射墙体几何不连续性的几何开口验证。该方法集成到vS-Graphs中作为概念验证。

Result: 在室内办公序列上的定性评估表明，该方法能够可靠地检测门道。

Conclusion: 该框架为在BIM（建筑信息模型）辅助的VSLAM中利用这些结构元素奠定了基础，源代码已开源。

Abstract: Doorways and passages are critical structural elements for indoor robot navigation, yet they remain underexplored in modern Visual SLAM (VSLAM) frameworks. This paper presents a passage-aware structural mapping approach for RGB-D VSLAM that detects doors and traversable openings by jointly fusing geometric, semantic, and topological cues. Doors are modeled as planar entities embedded within walls and classified as traversable or non-traversable based on their coplanarity with the supporting wall. Passages are inferred through two complementary strategies: traversal evidence accumulated from camera-wall interactions across consecutive keyframes, and geometric opening validation based on discontinuities in the mapped wall geometry. The proposed method is integrated into vS-Graphs as a proof of concept, enriching its scene graph with passage-level abstractions and improving room connectivity modeling. Qualitative evaluations on indoor office sequences demonstrate reliable doorway detection, and the framework lays the foundation for exploiting these elements in BIM-informed VSLAM. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/snt-arg/visual_sgraphs/tree/doorway_integration.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Super-Chevalley Restriction and Relative Lie Algebra Cohomology over the 2|3 Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23549)
*Chi-Ming Chang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究约化李代数 $\mathfrak g$ 对应的当前李超代数 $\mathfrak g[A]$ 的相对李代数上同调 $H^\bullet(\mathfrak g[A],\mathfrak g;\mathbb{C})$，发现三个有限秩现象：超交换限制映射对 $\mathfrak{so}_7$ 不是同构（存在非Cartan类阻碍）；对 $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ 和 $\mathfrak{so}_7$ 存在显式偶然类，反驳了类型A Loday-Quillen-Tsygan定理所暗示的朴素稳定像期望；Langlands对偶对 $(\mathfrak{so}_7,\mathfrak{sp}_6)$ 的经典相对上同调不同构。最后提出量子形变微分恢复对偶性的猜想，并给出 $\mathfrak{so}_7$ 中偶然类与非Cartan类配对的一阶证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Chang--Yin 在 $\mathcal N=4$ 超杨-米尔斯理论中弱耦合 $1/16$-BPS 上同调描述的启发，研究当前李超代数的相对李代数上同调结构。

Method: 定义超代数 $A:=\mathbb{C}[z_+,z_-]\otimes Λ(θ_1,θ_2,θ_3)$ 及当前李超代数 $\mathfrak g[A]$，计算其相对李代数上同调，通过具体实例 $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ 和 $\mathfrak{so}_7$ 分析超交换限制映射、稳定像期望及Langlands对偶性。

Result: 发现三个有限秩现象：超交换限制映射对 $\mathfrak{so}_7$ 不是同构；存在显式偶然类反驳朴素稳定像期望；Langlands对偶对 $(\mathfrak{so}_7,\mathfrak{sp}_6)$ 的经典相对上同调不同构。

Conclusion: 提出量子形变微分恢复对偶性的猜想，并给出 $\mathfrak{so}_7$ 中偶然类与非Cartan类配对的一阶证据。

Abstract: Let $A:=\mathbb{C}[z_+,z_-]\otimes Λ(θ_1,θ_2,θ_3)$, with $z_\pm$ even and $θ_1,θ_2,θ_3$ odd. For a reductive Lie algebra $\mathfrak g$, let $\mathfrak g[A]:=\mathfrak g\otimes A$ be the corresponding current Lie superalgebra. Motivated by the Chang--Yin description of weak-coupling $1/16$-BPS cohomology in $\mathcal N=4$ super-Yang--Mills, we study the relative Lie algebra cohomology $H^\bullet(\mathfrak g[A],\mathfrak g;\mathbb{C})$. We isolate three finite-rank phenomena. First, the natural $3|2$ super-commuting restriction map, viewed as a super analogue of Chevalley restriction and its commuting-scheme variants, already fails to be an isomorphism for $\mathfrak g=\mathfrak{so}_7$; the obstruction is a non-Cartan class. Second, the same algebra produces explicit fortuitous classes for $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ and $\mathfrak{so}_7$, giving concrete counterexamples to naive stable-image expectations suggested by the type-A Loday--Quillen--Tsygan theorem and its current-algebra refinements. Third, the classical relative cohomologies for the Langlands-dual pair $(\mathfrak{so}_7,\mathfrak{sp}_6)$ are not isomorphic. We then record the conjectural quantum deformation of the differential expected to restore duality, together with first-order evidence pairing the fortuitous and non-Cartan $\mathfrak{so}_7$ classes.

</details>


### [144] [On classical doubling method gamma factors for certain depth zero representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24713)
*Johannes Girsch,Elad Zelingher*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在有限李型经典群上定义了与Lapid-Rallis倍增法gamma因子类似的gamma因子，证明了其乘性，在非共轭对偶特征情形下给出了基于Deligne-Lusztig数据的显式公式，并将其与Lapid-Rallis的局部构造通过深度零超尖表示联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Piatetski-Shapiro-Rallis发现的经典群自守表示L-函数的积分表示构造（倍增法）及其Lapid-Rallis局部因子推广到有限群情形，建立有限群与局部域情形的联系。

Method: 在有限李型经典群上仿照Lapid-Rallis的框架定义倍增法gamma因子，利用Yost-Wolff-Zelingher的结果在非共轭对偶特征情形下给出基于Deligne-Lusztig数据的显式公式，并通过深度零超尖表示建立与Lapid-Rallis局部构造的联系。

Result: 证明了该gamma因子的乘性；在非共轭对偶特征情形下给出了基于Deligne-Lusztig数据的显式公式；建立了与Lapid-Rallis局部构造的联系。

Conclusion: 成功将倍增法gamma因子从自守表示和局部域情形推广到有限群情形，为有限群表示论与自守形式理论之间建立了新的联系。

Abstract: Piatetski-Shapiro--Rallis discovered an integral representation construction, known as the doubling method, for the tensor product $L$-function of a cuspidal automorphic representation of $G \times \mathrm{GL}_1$, where $G$ is a classical group. Lapid--Rallis defined and studied the counterpart local factors. In this article, following Lapid--Rallis, we define and study an analogous doubling method gamma factor associated to irreducible representations of classical finite groups of Lie type. We prove that this gamma factor is multiplicative and use results of Yost-Wolff--Zelingher to give explicit formulas for it in terms of the Deligne--Lusztig data of the representation in the non-conjugate-dual character case. Finally, we relate our construction to the local construction of Lapid--Rallis via certain depth zero supercuspidal representations of classical groups.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Lattice point visibility along powers of polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23050)
*Abraham Lobzenz,Tristan Phillips*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了沿多项式视线的格点可见性，并在多项式大范围内证明了Chaubey和Pandey提出的可见性密度猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在解决Chaubey和Pandey提出的可见性密度猜想，该猜想涉及多项式视线下格点可见性的密度分布问题。

Method: 论文采用数论分析方法，对多项式视线下的格点可见性进行系统性研究，通过构造特定的数学框架来证明猜想在一大类多项式中的成立性。

Result: 成功证明了Chaubey和Pandey的可见性密度猜想对于一大类多项式成立。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了格点可见性理论在多项式视线情形下的理解，为后续研究提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: We study lattice point visibility along polynomial lines of sight and prove the Visibility Density Conjecture of Chaubey and Pandey for a large class of polynomials.

</details>


### [146] [On the integrality of modular functions over $\mathbb{Z}[j]$ and Kronecker-type congruences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23096)
*Ho Yun Jung,Ja Kyung Koo,Dong Hwa Shin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：若素数 p ≡ ±1 (mod N) 且亚纯模函数 f（水平 N，有理 Fourier 系数）在 Z[j] 上整，则 (1/p)(f_p^p - f)(f_p - f^p) 也在 Z[j] 上整。这推广了 j 函数的经典 Kronecker 同余关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广经典的 Kronecker 同余关系（针对 j 函数）到更一般的亚纯模函数，研究这类函数在特定素数变换下的整性保持性质。

Method: 设 f 为水平 N、有理 Fourier 系数的亚纯模函数，p 为满足 p ≡ ±1 (mod N) 的素数。定义 f_p(τ)=f(τ/p)。在 f 在 Z[j] 上整的条件下，证明表达式 (1/p)(f_p^p - f)(f_p - f^p) 也在 Z[j] 上整。

Result: 证明了对于满足 p ≡ ±1 (mod N) 的素数 p，若 f 在 Z[j] 上整，则 (1/p)(f_p^p - f)(f_p - f^p) 在 Z[j] 上整。

Conclusion: 该结果将经典的 j 函数的 Kronecker 同余关系推广到了一类更广泛的亚纯模函数上，揭示了这类函数在素数变换下的代数整性保持性质。

Abstract: Let $N$ be a positive integer and let $f$ be a meromorphic modular function of level $N$ with rational Fourier coefficients. For a prime $p$, define a function $f_p$ on the complex upper half-plane $\mathbb{H}$ by \begin{equation*} f_p(τ)=f\left(\fracτ{p}\right)\quad(τ\in\mathbb{H}). \end{equation*} Let $j$ be the elliptic modular function. We show that if $p\equiv 1$ or $-1\Mod{N}$ and $f$ is integral over $\mathbb{Z}[j]$, then \begin{equation*} \frac{1}{p}(f_p^p-f)(f_p-f^p) \end{equation*} is also integral over $\mathbb{Z}[j]$. This result generalizes the classical Kronecker congruence relation for $j$.

</details>


### [147] [A Proof of Bala's General-$m$ Representation of the Harmonic Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23206)
*Tong Niu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于调和数 $H_n$ 的恒等式，该恒等式对任意非零整数 $m$ 成立，此前由 P. Bala 在 2022 年提出猜想。证明方法通过变量替换 $u = x/(1-x)^m$，将其转化为两个形式幂级数恒等式，并利用 Lagrange–Bürmann 公式和不动点性质完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明 P. Bala 于 2022 年在 OEIS 中提出的关于调和数 $H_n$ 的恒等式猜想，该猜想将经典情形 ($m=1,2$) 推广到任意非零整数 $m$。

Method: 在 $\mathbb{Q}[[x]]$ 中工作，通过变量替换 $u = x/(1-x)^m$，将原恒等式转化为两个形式幂级数恒等式：一是利用 Lagrange–Bürmann 公式计算 $\sum_{k\ge1} \binom{mk}{k} u^k / k$；二是利用不动点性质，即方程 $v = u(1-v)^m$ 的唯一解 $v(u)=x$。论证可自然推广到任意非零复数 $m$。

Result: 成功证明了该恒等式对任意非零整数 $m$ 成立，且论证可推广到任意非零复数 $m$。

Conclusion: 该恒等式得到彻底证明，且证明方法具有一般性，不限于整数 $m$。

Abstract: For every nonzero integer $m$ and every integer $n \ge 1$, the $n$\textsuperscript{th} harmonic number $H_n = 1 + \tfrac12 + \dots + \tfrac1n$ satisfies the identity \[
  H_n \;=\; \frac{1}{m}\,\sum_{k=1}^{n} \frac{(-1)^{k+1}}{k}\,
  \binom{m k}{k}\binom{n + (m-1)k}{n - k}. \] The cases $m = 1$ and $m = 2$ are classical; for general nonzero integer $m$ the identity was conjectured by P.~Bala in the OEIS entry A001008 in 2022 and remained open. We prove it here, working throughout in $\mathbb{Q}[[x]]$. The proof reduces, via a substitution $u = x/(1-x)^m$, to two formal-power-series identities: a Lagrange--Bürmann evaluation of $\sum_{k\ge1} \binom{mk}{k} u^k / k$, and the fixed-point fact that under that substitution the unique solution $v(u)$ of $v = u(1-v)^{m}$ is $v = x$. The argument extends verbatim to arbitrary complex $m \ne 0$.

</details>


### [148] [Core abaci and Diophantine equations I: fundamental weight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23311)
*Yanbo Li,Jiansheng Zhang,Shasha Zhu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文定义了任意电荷的经典仿射型核心算盘，极大扩展了零电荷核心的概念，并用其参数化仿射Grassmannian。通过将核心算盘与权重及仿射Weyl群元素关联，证明了β的高度等于w的原子长度，解决了Brunat等人提出的广义公开问题。此外，建立了经典仿射型的Diophantine方程，并给出了特定核心算盘数量的封闭计算公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展零电荷核心概念至任意电荷，以参数化经典仿射型的仿射Grassmannian，并解决Brunat、Chapelier-Laget和Gerber提出的公开问题的广义版本。

Method: 定义任意电荷的经典仿射型核心算盘；将核心算盘(λ,j)与权重Λ_j-β及仿射Weyl群元素w_{λ,j}关联；利用Uglov向量给出的高度公式建立Diophantine方程。

Result: 证明了β的高度等于w_{λ,j}的原子长度；建立了经典仿射型的Diophantine方程并证明其解可由核心算盘完全参数化；给出了计算特定种类核心算盘数量的封闭公式。

Conclusion: 核心算盘是参数化仿射Grassmannian的有效工具，能解决高度与原子长度关系的公开问题，并为相关Diophantine方程提供完整参数化方案。

Abstract: In the light of a series of papers on moving vectors, we define and study core abaci of classical affine types for arbitrary charge. This greatly extends the concept of cores with charge zero, and make us being able to parameterize the affine Grassmannian $W^j$ by core abaci of charge $j$ for arbitrary classical affine types. By associating a core abacus $(\lam, j)$ to a weight $Λ_j-β$ and an affine Weyl group element $w_{\lam, j}$, we prove that the height of $β$ is equal to the atomic length of $w_{\lam, j}$. This solves a generalized version of the open problem raised by Brunat, Chapelier-Laget and Gerber. Moreover, Diophantine equations of classical affine types are established by using the height formula that given by Uglov vector. The solutions of certain classes of these Diophantine equations are proved to be completely parameterised by core abaci. As another application, closed formulae for computing the number of certain kinds of core abaci are given.

</details>


### [149] [Hybrid Conjecture in a Mixed Shimura variety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23376)
*Rodolphe Richard,Andrei Yafaev*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了混合Shimura簇中的混合混合猜想，以通用阿贝尔概形$\mathcal{A}_g\to \mathbb{A}_g$为例，采用均衡分布与o-极小性相结合的新方法，证明了该猜想在$\mathbb{A}_g$上蕴含André-Pink-Zannier、André-Oort、混合André-Oort、Manin-Mumford及Mordell-Lang等经典猜想，并得到了数论和伽罗瓦理论方面的推广结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者先前在阿贝尔型Shimura簇上证明了统一André-Pink-Zannier猜想与André-Oort猜想的混合猜想，本文旨在将此结果推广至混合Shimura簇，并以通用阿贝尔概形$\mathcal{A}_g\to \mathbb{A}_g$作为核心例子进行验证。

Method: 摒弃传统的Pila-Zannier策略，采用均衡分布（equidistribution）与o-极小性（o-minimality）相结合的新方法。

Result: (1) 证明了$\mathbb{A}_g$上的混合猜想（含André-Pink-Zannier和André-Oort）；(2) 证明了$\mathcal{A}_g$上的混合André-Oort猜想；(3) 证明了任意阿贝尔簇的Manin-Mumford猜想；(4) 得到了Baldi-Richard-Ullmo的"算术束中的Manin-Mumford"结果的阿贝尔概形类比；(5) 将$\mathbb{A}_g$上的混合混合猜想简化为其"mordellic"部分；(6) 证明了Ribet关于阿贝尔簇的Kummer理论的均匀变体及Serre关于Lang猜想的定理等伽罗瓦理论结果。

Conclusion: 混合猜想在混合Shimura簇情形下成立，且统一了多个数论与算术几何中的核心猜想，新方法（均衡分布+o-极小性）为该领域提供了强有力的替代工具。

Abstract: The authors previously formulated the hybrid conjecture, unifying André-Pink-Zannier and André-Oort conjectures, and proved it in Shimura varieties of abelian type. We study its analogue for mixed Shimura varieties, and consider the prime example, the universal abelian scheme $\mathcal{A}_g\to \mathbb{A}_g$.
  In a radical departure from the Pila-Zannier strategy, typically applied to such questions, we employ instead a combination of equidistribution and o-minimality
  Our main result strictly includes the following: the Hybrid Conjecture, in particular the André-Pink-Zannier and André-Oort conjectures, for $\mathbb{A}_g$; the mixed André-Oort conjecture for $\mathcal{A}_g$; and Manin-Mumford conjecture for arbitrary abelian varieties. It also yields an analogue of the ``Manin-Mumford in arithmetic pencil", a result of Baldi-Richard-Ullmo, for abelian schemes over a variety.
  The mixed hybrid conjecture in $\mathcal{A}_g$ also encompasses the Mordell-Lang conjecture. We actually reduce the mixed hybrid conjecture for $\mathbb{A}_g$ to its "mordellic" part.
  We also prove, Galois-theoretic results: uniform variants on the Ribet's Kummer theory of Abelian varieties, and Serre's theorem on Lang's conjecture.

</details>


### [150] [Differences of squares of upper-triangular $2\times 2$ integer matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23404)
*Andrej Dujella,Zrinka Franušić*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文刻画了上三角整矩阵M=\begin{pmatrix}p&r\\0&q\end{pmatrix}可表示为两个上三角整矩阵A、B的平方差A²-B²的条件，给出了基于p和q的平方差表示以及r的可整除性的完整判据，并进一步给出了用p、q、r的同余条件表示的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究上三角整矩阵能否表示为两个上三角整矩阵的平方差，这是矩阵理论中一类经典表示问题在特殊矩阵子集上的推广。

Method: 将问题转化为关于对角元p、q的平方差表示问题，并分析非对角元r在可表示性下的约束条件，最终获得同余刻画。

Result: 给出了上三角整矩阵可表示为平方差的充要条件，包括p和q的平方差表示条件以及r的附加可整除条件，以及基于同余条件的完整分类。

Conclusion: 该结果完整解决了上三角整矩阵作为两个上三角整矩阵平方差的表示问题，并且分类简洁，仅依赖于p、q、r的同余性质。

Abstract: We consider the problem of characterizing upper-triangular matrices $M=\begin{pmatrix}p&r\\0&q\end{pmatrix}\in M_2(\mathbb Z)$ which can be represented in the form $A^2-B^2$ with upper-triangular integer matrices $A$ and $B$ and give a complete criterion in terms of representations of $p$ and $q$ as differences of two squares and an additional divisibility condition on $r$. Also, we give a complete classification of representable matrices in terms of congruence conditions on $p$, $q$, and $r$.

</details>


### [151] [On (not) learning the Möbius function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23427)
*Alexey Pozdnyakov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在学习Möbius或Liouville函数时，多种标准学习技术（包括核方法、噪声梯度方法和相关统计查询算法）的下界。这些下界源于Möbius函数与不同有限阿贝尔群上数字特征的相关性定量界，具体群结构由算法输入数据类型决定。研究还指出这类下界与某些数字素数定理密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究标准学习技术（核方法、噪声梯度方法、相关统计查询算法）在学习数论中的Möbius或Liouville函数时的局限性，并从理论上给出下界保证。

Method: 通过建立Möbius函数与有限阿贝尔群上数字特征之间的相关性定量界限来推导下界。具体地，使用不同素数模（对应循环群）和固定素数的基p展开（对应初等阿贝尔p-群）两种数据结构。

Result: 得到了核方法、噪声梯度方法和相关统计查询算法在学习Möbius/Liouville函数时的下界，并发现这些下界与某些数字素数定理有密切联系。

Conclusion: 标准学习技术在Möbius/Liouville函数学习任务中存在本质局限性，同时建立了计算学习理论与数论中数字素数定理之间的新联系。

Abstract: We prove lower bounds on learning the Möbius or Liouville function with a variety of standard learning techniques, including kernel methods, noisy gradient methods, and correlational statistical query algorithms. These results follow from quantitative bounds on the correlation of Möbius with digital characters of various finite abelian groups, where the group is dictated by the type of input data the algorithm is given. Using residues mod $p$ for many different primes corresponds to a cyclic group, and using the base $p$ expansion for a fixed prime corresponds to an elementary abelian $p$-group. We also note that lower bounds of this form are closely related to certain types of digital prime number theorems.

</details>


### [152] [A necessary condition for a congruent number of the form $8k+3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23450)
*Shamik Das,Sudipa Mondal*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类特定形式的无平方因子整数n（由模8余1的素数pi和模8余3的素数q乘积构成）作为同余数时，其虚二次域Q(√-n)与Q(√-p1p2...pt)的类数2-部分之间模2的幂次的同余关系，并利用修正的Rédei矩阵给出了证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同余数问题是一个古老的数论难题，此前关于同余数与类数之间关系的研究较多，但对于这种特定素数分解形式的同余数，其二次域类数2-部分之间的精确同余关系尚未明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用修正的Rédei矩阵作为主要分析工具，对满足特定同余条件的素数乘积形式的同余数n，建立其与简化形式（不含模8余3的素数因子）的虚二次域类数2-部分之间的同余关系。

Result: 得到了在n为同余数的假设下，Q(√-n)与Q(√-p1p2...pt)的类数2-部分之间模2的幂次的同余关系。

Conclusion: 该结果进一步揭示了同余数与虚二次域类数结构之间的深层联系，为同余数的数域判别提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: A positive square-free integer is called a \textit{congruent number} if it arises as the area of a right triangle with rational side lengths. Let $ n = p_1p_2 \cdots p_t q $ be a square-free integer, where each $ p_i \equiv 1 \pmod{8} $ and $ q \equiv 3 \pmod{8} $, with the $ p_i $ and $ q $ being distinct primes. In this article, we present a congruence relation modulo powers of 2 between the 2-part of the class numbers of $ \mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-n}) $ and $ \mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-p_1p_2 \cdots p_t}) $, under the assumption that $ n $ is a congruent number, using a modified Rédei matrix.

</details>


### [153] [$2$-Selmer groups, $2$-class groups, and congruent numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23482)
*Shamik Das,Debajyoti De,Sudipa Mondal*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了特定平方自由整数为同余数的必要条件，通过虚二次域类数的可除性质，给出了两类形如 n = p₁p₂...p_t q 的整数（其中 p_i ≡ 5 mod 8，q ≡ 7 mod 8 或 3 mod 8）在同余数条件下的类数约束条件，并提供了非同余数的定量下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索特定形式的平方自由整数是否为同余数这一经典数论问题，利用类数性质建立新的判别条件。

Method: 利用虚二次域 Q(√-n) 的类数的可除性质；对两类不同形式的整数分别分析，通过类数的模2幂同余关系建立约束条件。

Result: 对于 n = p₁p₂...p_t q，p_i ≡ 5 mod 8，q ≡ 7 mod 8 情形，若 n 为同余数则类数 h(-n) 满足特定可除条件，并给出非同余数的定量下界；对于 q ≡ 3 mod 8 情形，得到了类数 h(-n) 与 h(-p₁p₂...p_t) 之间的模2幂同余关系。

Conclusion: 建立了新的同余数判别条件，为研究该类数论问题提供了有效工具。

Abstract: In this article, we study necessary conditions for certain square-free integers to be congruent numbers. Our method uses divisibility properties of class numbers of related imaginary quadratic fields. We first consider positive square-free integers of the form $n = p_1 p_2 \cdots p_t q,$ where each prime $p_i \equiv 5 \pmod{8}$ and $q \equiv 7 \pmod{8}$. We show that if such an integer $n$ is a congruent number, then the class number $h(-n)$ of the quadratic field $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-n})$ satisfies a specific divisibility condition. Furthermore, we provide quantitative lower bounds on the number of non-congruent numbers of this form. Next, we study integers of the form $n = p_1 p_2 \cdots p_t q,$ with $p_i \equiv 5 \pmod{8}$ and $q \equiv 3 \pmod{8}$. Assuming that $n$ is a congruent number, we obtain a congruence modulo powers of $2$ between the class numbers of the fields $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-n})$ and $\mathbb{Q}\!\left(\sqrt{-p_1 p_2 \cdots p_t}\right)$.

</details>


### [154] [The Absolute Anabelian Geometry of Virtual Curves of Arbitrary Genus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23485)
*Zeming Sun*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在前期工作基础上，将尖点虚拟曲线（pointed virtual curves）的anabelian研究从亏格零情形推广到任意亏格曲线。为此引入了CAVC型包含（群论概念）和虚拟解尖化（virtual decuspidaloid，范畴论概念），并建立了判断虚拟曲线“几何性”的准则，给出了算术基本群截面来自有理点的群论条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将前期在亏格零情形下建立的关于Galois截面的基本群论拉回（pointed virtual curves）的anabelian结果推广到任意亏格曲线，深化对anabelian几何中截面问题的理解。

Method: 引入群论概念“CAVC型包含”和范畴论概念“虚拟解尖化（virtual decuspidaloid）”，以此作为工具实现从亏格零到任意亏格的技术推广。

Result: 成功将anabelian核心结果从亏格零推广到任意亏格曲线；建立了判断虚拟曲线几何性的准则，给出了算术基本群截面源于有理点的群论充分条件。

Conclusion: 通过引入CAVC型包含和虚拟解尖化等新概念，实现了对任意亏格曲线情形下pointed virtual curves的anabelian刻画，并为截面问题的几何性判定提供了群论判据。

Abstract: The objective of this paper is to further study the anabelian object referred to as \emph{pointed virtual curves}. Building upon previous work that investigated these fundamental-group-theoretic pullbacks of Galois sections in the genus-zero situation, we extend the central anabelian results to curves of arbitrary genus. To facilitate this generalization, we introduce the group-theoretic notion of an inclusion of CAVC-type and the categorical-theoretic notion of a virtual decuspidaloid. Furthermore, we establish a criterion regarding the "geometricity" of certain virtual curves, providing group-theoretic conditions under which a section of an arithmetic fundamental group arises from a rational point.

</details>


### [155] [Picking up the partial sums of the Möbius function problem with probabilistic number theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23517)
*Maxie Dion Schmidt*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了几种混合乘性-加性函数，并通过概率假设探索了经典数论函数（如莫比乌斯函数和利乌维尔函数）部分和的渐近增长，提出了一种比依赖黎曼假设或ζ(s)零点线性独立假设更易于证明的|M(x)|/√x极限增长证明方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望找到一种比传统依赖黎曼假设或ζ(s)非平凡零点线性独立假设更易于严格化的途径，来证明|M(x)|/√x的极限渐近增长。

Method: 通过重新审视几种混合乘性-加性函数（其狄利克雷生成函数与素zeta函数P(s)相关），利用Ω(n)和μ²(n)在n≤x时取值独立性的概率假设，对前期手稿中的恒等式进行求和。

Result: 恢复了|M(x)|/√x极限渐近增长的证明，其假设条件比以往依赖黎曼假设或ζ(s)零点线性独立的结果更易于严格化。

Conclusion: 通过概率假设方法，可以为经典数论函数的渐近行为提供更易于严格证明的新途径，降低了对强未证明假设的依赖。

Abstract: We revisit several hybrid multiplicative-to-additive type functions from a recent preprint article. These functions, $g(n)$ with Dirichlet generating function (DGF) $ζ(s)^{-1} (1+P(s))^{-1}$ for $\Re(s) > 1$ where $P(s) = \sum_p p^{-s}$ is the prime zeta function, $|g(n)| = λ(n) g(n)$ with DGF $ζ(2s)^{-1}(1-P(s))^{-1}$, and $C_Ω(n)$ with DGF $(1-P(s))^{-1}$. Each of these function variants are defined in terms of the additive (respectively, strongly additive) functions $ω(n)$ and $Ω(n)$. These two auxiliary functions are used in the prior manuscript to relate partial sums of the classical Möbius function, $μ(n)$, to signed partial sums involving the prime counting function, $π(x)$, and the Liouville lambda function, $λ(n) := (-1)^{Ω(n)}$.
  In this article, we explore summing the identities from the first manuscript using several probabilistic assumptions about the independence of the values of $Ω(n)$ and $μ^2(n)$ for $n \leq x$ at large $x$. We recover proofs of the limiting asymptotic growth of $|M(x)| / \sqrt{x}$ whose hypotheses promise to be substantially more attainable to make rigorous than past results from other authors relying on the Riemann Hypothesis or assumption of the linear independence of the simple, non-trivial zeros of $ζ(s)$.

</details>


### [156] [Large sieve inequality for sums of Legendre symbols over short intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23661)
*Igor Shparlinski,Yixiu Xiao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用Burgess界和Selberg筛法，获得了区间[u+1, u+h]上Legendre符号和对素数p（在dyadic区间[Q,2Q]中）求和的第二矩的上界。该界是非平凡的，对任何u ≤ Q，只要h ≥ ψ(Q)（其中ψ(Q)→∞当Q→∞），就给出关于h的幂次节省。这推广了Heath-Brown (1995)关于初始区间[1,h]上二次特征和矩的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Heath-Brown关于初始区间[1,h]上二次特征和矩的结果推广到一般区间[u+1, u+h]上，研究任意位置区间上Legendre符号和的矩估计。

Method: 结合Burgess界（用于处理特征和的上界）与Selberg筛法（用于对素数进行加权求和），分析区间[u+1, u+h]上Legendre符号和的第二矩。

Result: 得到了第二矩的非平凡上界，在h ≥ ψ(Q)（ψ(Q)→∞）且任意u ≤ Q的条件下，对h实现了幂次节省。

Conclusion: 成功将Heath-Brown的结果从初始区间推广到任意区间，证明了只要区间长度足够大（趋于无穷），即使区间位置任意，同样具有幂次节省效果。

Abstract: We use the Burgess bound and Selberg sieve to obtain an upper bound on the second moment of sums over an interval $[u+1,u+h]$ of Legendre symbols modulo primes $p$ in a dyadic interval $[Q,2Q]$. The bound is nontrivial and gives a power saving with respect to $h$ for any $u \le Q$, provided $h \ge ψ(Q)$ for any function $ψ(Q)\to\infty$ as $Q\to\infty$. This can be viewed as a generalisation of a result of D. R. Heath-Brown (1995) on moments of sums or quadratic characters over the initial interval $[1,h]$.

</details>


### [157] [Unbounded logarithmic limsup in Erdős problem 684](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23784)
*Ji Ho Bae*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Erdős问题684中函数f(n)（即使得二项式系数{n choose k}的k-光滑部分超过n²的最小k），通过构造整数n使得f(n)大于log n的任意常数倍，证明了limsup f(n)/log n = ∞。构造使用了基于L_M=lcm(1,...,M)的短乘子筛、Q_M=M!/L_M盒子构造、dyadic固定Ω乘积壳局部化，以及Timofeev的大Ω均值进级数方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Erdős问题684中关于f(n)增长性的未决问题，证明f(n)/log n的上极限为无穷大，即f(n)的增长速度可以超过任意常数倍的log n。

Method: 采用短乘子筛（基于L_M=lcm(1,...,M)）、Q_M=M!/L_M盒子构造、dyadic固定Ω乘积壳局部化，以及Timofeev的大Ω均值进级数方法的局部化低截断形式。

Result: 对于每个固定C>1，存在无穷多个n使得f(n) > (C-o(1)) log n，从而limsup_{n→∞} f(n)/log n = ∞。

Conclusion: f(n)的增长速度可以超过任意常数倍的log n，证明了其上极限为无穷大。

Abstract: Let $f(n)$ be the least $k$ for which the $k$-smooth part of ${n \choose k}$ exceeds $n^2$, as in Erdős problem 684. We construct integers $n$ for which $f(n)$ is larger than any prescribed constant multiple of $\log n$. More precisely, for every fixed $C>1$, we obtain infinitely many $n$ such that $f(n)>(C-o(1))\log n$. Hence $$ \limsup_{n\to\infty} f(n)/\log n=\infty. $$ The construction uses a short-multiplier sieve built from $L_M=\mathrm{lcm}(1,\ldots,M)$, a $Q_M=M!/L_M$ box formulation, and a dyadic fixed-$Ω$ product-shell localization. The analytic input is Timofeev's large-$Ω$ mean-in-progressions method, used in a localized lower-cutoff form.

</details>


### [158] [On optimization on ravine functions. Minkowski-Cohn moduli surface in Cohn parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23871)
*Nikolaj M. Glazunov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文以Minkowski-Cohn模曲面为例，从优化视角简要概述了沟壑函数，展示了点的最小化问题的表示与求解要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过Minkowski-Cohn模曲面这一具体实例，从优化角度理解沟壑函数（ravine functions）的特性，为更复杂的优化问题提供理论基础。

Method: 以Minkowski-Cohn模曲面为案例，从优化视角分析沟壑函数，展示在特定点上最小化问题的表示与求解过程。

Result: 给出了在Minkowski-Cohn模曲面上沟壑函数的优化表示以及点最小化问题的求解要素。

Conclusion: 沟壑函数在Minkowski-Cohn模曲面上的优化视角分析有助于理解该类函数的几何与优化性质。

Abstract: This paper presents a brief overview of ravine functions using the example of the Minkowski-Cohn moduli surface from the point of view of optimization on it. Elements of representation and solution of the minimization problem at a point are presented.

</details>


### [159] [Non-Vanishing of Cubic Twists of $GL_n(\mathbb{Q})$ $L$-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23900)
*Sayan Ghosh,Pratim Mitra*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于GL_n(Q)上不可约、尖点的自守表示π（n≥3，n=3时需缓增），以及特定实部范围外的复数s，存在无穷多个三次本原狄利克雷特征χ使得L(s,π×χ)非零。此前类似结果仅适用于无阶限制的本原狄利克雷特征和二次特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自守L-函数在扭曲下的非消没性，特别是针对三次狄利克雷特征（即阶数等于3）的情形，填补此前仅对一般本原特征或二次特征已知的结果空白。

Method: 采用解析数论与自守形式的工具，通过构造合适的零点检测函数、谱分解以及L-函数的性质分析，在特定条件下证明存在无穷多个三次特征使得扭曲L-函数非零。

Result: 对于n≥3的不可约尖点自守表示π（n=3时要求缓增），当s的实部处于特定区间之外时，存在无穷多个三次本原狄利克雷特征χ使得L(s,π×χ)≠0。

Conclusion: 将自守L-函数扭曲非消没性的已知结果从一般本原特征和二次特征推广到了三次特征，推进了对高阶特征扭曲下L-函数零点分布的理解。

Abstract: Let $π$ be an irreducible, cuspidal automorphic representation of $GL_n(\mathbb{A}_\mathbb{Q})$ ($n\geq 3$), which is tempered only for $n=3$. Let $s$ be a complex number such that $\Re(s)\notin \left[1/n, 1-1/n\right]$ if $n\neq 4$; $\Re(s)\notin\left[1/5, 4/5\right]$ if $n=4$, then we show that there are infinitely many primitive cubic Dirichlet characters $χ$ such that $L(s,π\times χ)\neq 0$. Similar results were previously known only for primitive Dirichlet characters without any restriction on the order and quadratic Dirichlet characters.

</details>


### [160] [On the Gauss circle problem over smooth numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23918)
*Peng Gao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了加权r(n)的y-光滑数之和Ψ_G(x,y)的渐近估值，其中r(n)是n表示为两平方和的方法数，并给出了在特定x≥y≥2范围内的渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将高斯圆问题（r(n)的加和）与光滑数分布相结合，探讨加权光滑数的渐近行为。

Method: 解析数论方法，可能涉及Perron公式、Dirichlet级数、鞍点分析或zeta函数技巧，结合光滑数筛法。

Result: 在特定x≥y≥2范围内给出了Ψ_G(x,y)的渐近公式。

Conclusion: 建立了高斯圆问题背景下加权光滑数和的渐近估值，推广了经典光滑数分布结果。

Abstract: The Gauss circle problem concerns with the evaluation of $\sum_{n \leq x}r(n)$, where $r(n)$ denotes the number of representations of $n$ as sums of two squares and $x \geq 2$. Let $Ψ_G(x,y)$ denote the sum of $y$-smooth numbers below $x$ weighted by $r(n)$. In this paper, we evaluate $Ψ_G(x,y)$ asymptotically for certain ranges of $x \geq y \geq 2$.

</details>


### [161] [On D-cap-Modules of Finite Length on Rigid Analytic Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24173)
*Julian Reichardt*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对拟紧光滑刚性解析空间，扩展函子将全纯D-模映为余容许D-cap-模，且作为弱全纯D-cap-模具有有限长度。作为核心工具，引入了完备Weyl代数上有限生成模的Hilbert多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拟紧光滑刚性解析空间上D-模的扩展函子性质，特别是全纯D-模在扩展后的有限长度性质，并统一处理Bode-Bitoun的亚纯联络与Ardakov-Bode-Wadsley的局部上同调群的结构性质。

Method: 引入并研究完备Weyl代数上有限生成模的Hilbert多项式作为核心工具，利用该工具分析扩展函子作用下的模结构。

Result: 扩展函子将全纯D-模映为余容许且作为弱全纯D-cap-模具有有限长度的模；进而得到亚纯联络与局部上同调群均为有限长度弱全纯D-cap-模。

Conclusion: 建立了刚性解析空间上D-模范畴的有限长度性质，为相关模结构分析提供了统一的工具与视角。

Abstract: We show that for quasi-compact smooth rigid analytic spaces, the extension functor sends holonomic D-modules to coadmissible D-cap-modules which are of finite length as weakly holonomic D-cap-modules. Using this, we show that the meromorphic connections considered by Bode--Bitoun and the local cohomology groups considered by Ardakov--Bode--Wadsley are of finite length as weakly holonomic D-cap-modules for quasi-compact smooth rigid analytic spaces. As a central tool, we introduce and study Hilbert polynomials for finitely generated modules over completed Weyl algebras.

</details>


### [162] [Linear Bounds for Differentiable Limits of Weak Pair Correlation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24481)
*Christian Weiß*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了弱对关联函数 $f_{N,\beta}(s)$ 的极限行为，在极限 $f_\beta(s)$ 存在且在原点附近可微的假设下，建立了 $2s \le f_\beta(s) \le f'_\beta(0)\, s$ 的界，为极限函数提供了通用约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱对关联函数的基本性质（单调性、零点极限）已被了解，但其极限函数的一般行为尚不明确。本文旨在探讨在极限存在且可微的条件下，该函数所满足的约束关系。

Method: 假设极限 $f_\beta(s)=\lim_{N\to\infty} f_{N,\beta}(s)$ 存在且在原点的邻域内可微，利用这些分析条件推导函数的上界和下界。

Result: 建立了不等式 $2s \le f_\beta(s) \le f'_\beta(0)\, s$，其中下界为 $2s$，上界由原点处的导数 $f'_\beta(0)$ 控制。

Conclusion: 本文为弱对关联函数的极限形式提供了普适的上下界估计，揭示了其线性增长行为，为进一步研究序列的均匀分布性质提供了分析工具。

Abstract: For $s \geq 0$ and a parameter $0 < β< 1$, the weak pair correlation function $f_{N,β}(s)$ for the first $N \in \mathbb{N}$ elements of a sequence $(x_n)_{n \in \mathbb{N}} \subset[0,1]$ is evidently non-decreasing in $s$. Moreover, it satisfies $\lim_{N \to \infty} f_{N,β}(0) = 0$ if the elements of $(x_n)_{n \in \mathbb{N}}$ are distinct. Beyond these basic observations, little is known in general about the behavior of the limiting function. In this note, we investigate the situation in which the limit $f_β(s)=\lim_{N\to\infty} f_{N,β}(s)$ exists for all $s\ge 0$ and is differentiable in a neighborhood of the origin. Under these assumptions, we establish the bounds $2s \le f_β(s) \le f'_β(0)\, s,$ thereby providing general constraints on the limiting function.

</details>


### [163] [On $k$-th power Diophantine triples of the form $\{a^k, b, c\}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24650)
*Clemens Fuchs,Miriam Schönauer*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明对于 k≥3 且 1<a^k<b<c，不存在形如 {a^k, b, c} 的 k 次幂丢番图三元组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究丢番图三元组问题，特别是形如 {a^k, b, c} 的 k 次幂形式的特殊情况，并探索其在 k≥3 时的存在性。

Method: 通过数学证明的方法，对 k≥3 且 1<a^k<b<c 的约束条件下，论证不存在这样的 k 次幂丢番图三元组。

Result: 成功证明了在给定条件下，不存在形如 {a^k, b, c} 的 k 次幂丢番图三元组。

Conclusion: 该结论填补了丢番图三元组在 k 次幂形式研究中的一个空白，为相关问题提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that there are no $k$-th power Diophantine triples of the form $\{a^k,b,c\}$ for $k\geq 3$ and $1<a^k<b<c$.

</details>


### [164] [Joint Sato-Tate Laws for Transformations of Hecke Eigenvalues: The Vertical Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24753)
*Mohammad H. Hamdar,Tian Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个框架，用于研究一大类联合等分布问题并给出有效误差项。该框架通过证明Erdös–Turán不等式的高维μ-类比，并利用Hardy-Krause变差理论，特别是发展了一种用有界H-K变差函数逼近相关函数的技巧。主要应用于尖点形式空间和有限域上椭圆曲线族的垂直Sato–Tate问题，获得了关于傅里叶系数和Frobenius迹的算术关系分布及多维函数分布的新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的等分布结果往往缺乏有效的误差估计，且对于多维联合分布问题缺乏统一处理框架。

Method: 证明高维μ-形式的Erdös–Turán不等式，结合Hardy-Krause变差理论，发展函数逼近技术。

Result: 获得了尖点形式空间和椭圆曲线族垂直Sato–Tate问题中算术关系分布及多维函数分布的有效误差估计新结果。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够统一处理一大类联合等分布问题并得到有效误差项，在数论等分布问题中有广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce a framework within which a large class of joint equidistribution problems can be studied and resolved with effective error terms. This involves proving a higher dimensional and $μ$-analogue of the Erdös-Turán inequality, and utilizing the theory of the Hardy-Krause (H-K) variation from analysis, where, in particular, we formulate a technique to approximate a broad class of relevant functions by functions of bounded H-K variation. Our main focus will be on the vertical Sato-Tate problem for spaces of cusp forms and for families of elliptic curves over finite fields. In particular, we obtain novel results concerning the distribution of arithmetic relations, and, more generally, multi-dimensional functions of Fourier coefficients and Frobenius traces.

</details>


### [165] [The asymptotic oscillations of moments related to Dirichlet series with missing digits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24754)
*Jean-François Burnol*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明，与基数b中缺失数字的zeta级数数值评估相关的单位区间上某些离散测度的（适当重新缩放的）矩，在索引的基数b对数下渐近1-周期，即索引乘以b时渐近不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究缺失数字的zeta级数数值评估中离散测度的矩的渐近行为，探索其在基数b对数尺度下的周期性结构。

Method: 通过数学证明，分析离散测度的矩在适当重新缩放后的渐近性质，建立其与基数b对数周期性的联系。

Result: 证明了这些矩在索引的基数b对数下具有渐近1-周期性，即索引乘以b时渐近保持不变。

Conclusion: 离散测度的矩在基数b对数尺度下表现出渐近周期行为，这为相关zeta级数的数值分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We prove that the (suitably rescaled) moments of certain discrete measures on the unit interval, which are related to the numerical evaluation of zeta series with missing digits in radix $b$, are asymptotically $1$-periodic in the base $b$ logarithm of the index, i.e. asymptotically invariant under multiplication by $b$ of the index.

</details>
